### [36] [Beyond Bag-of-Patches: Learning Global Layout via Textual Supervision for Late-Interaction Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08421)
*Pascal Tilli,Mohsen Mesgar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉文档检索(VDR)模型主要依赖后期交互架构、优先考虑局部相似性而忽略全局布局结构的问题，本文提出一种可学习文档布局的多模态编码器，通过注入全局布局嵌入来增强局部块表示，在四个ViDoRe-v2数据集上相比最强基线ColPali/ColQwen实现nDCG@5提升2.4、MAP@5提升2.3的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VDR模型采用后期交互架构，用局部块嵌入表示文档并与查询令牌匹配，这种架构优先考虑局部相似性而忽略了文档的全局布局结构（如图表、表格、文本的混合布局），导致检索错误。

Method: 提出一种多模态编码器，在不改变推理过程的前提下，通过融合全局布局嵌入来增强局部块表示。该全局布局嵌入通过编码文档布局信息的文本描述进行训练，使布局信息可学习。

Result: 在四个ViDoRe-v2数据集上，相比最强架构可比基线ColPali/ColQwen，模型在nDCG@5上提升2.4，MAP@5上提升2.3，且每个数据集的提升均具有统计显著性。

Conclusion: 注入全局布局嵌入能有效提升VDR模型的检索性能，证明布局结构信息对文档检索的重要性，且该方法不改变推理过程，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Visual Document Retrieval (VDR) models mostly rely on late interaction architectures, in which documents are represented by a set of local patch embeddings and then matched against query tokens. While efficient, this architecture prioritizes local similarity over global layout structure of documents to estimate relevancy between documents and query. In practice, this leads to errors as relevance originates from layout structure of documents with heterogeneous layouts combining figures, tables, and text. We make document layout learnable without changing inference. We propose a multimodal encoder that augments local patch representations with a global layout embedding, trained via textual descriptions encoding document layout information. Across four ViDoRe-v2 datasets, our model improves over the strongest architecturally comparable ColPali/ColQwen baseline by +2.4 nDCG@5 and +2.3 MAP@5, with statistically significant per-dataset gains over ColQwen.

</details>


### [37] [NICE FACT: Diagnosing and Calibrating VLMs in Quantitative Reasoning for Kinematic Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08452)
*Jian Lan,Zhicheng Liu,Xinpeng Wang,Yuhao Zhou,Haokun Chen,Jiancheng Lv,Barbara Plank,Thomas Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NICE和FACT双诊断范式，系统分析VLM在运动物理推理中的视觉保真度、物理定律理解和时间 grounding 能力，发现现有模型无法识别视觉前提或利用必要物理定律来得出答案，并引入邻域感知校准方法评估和提升置信度可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM在空间智能任务（如物理推理）中表现不佳，社区缺乏科学分析来揭示模型是忠实推理还是似是而非地猜测，亟需系统性诊断方法。

Method: 提出NICE和FACT双诊断范式：FACT分解诊断视觉保真度、物理定律理解和时间 grounding；NICE提出邻域感知校准方法及新指标来评估和校准置信度可靠性。

Result: 在6个最新SOTA VLM上实验发现，模型无法识别视觉前提条件，也无法利用必要的物理定律来得出答案。

Conclusion: 建立了标准化诊断范式，指导开发忠实且具有物理基础的VLM。

Abstract: The ability to derive precise spatial and physical insights is a cornerstone of vision-language models (VLMs), yet their poor performances in related spatial intelligence tasks such as physical reasoning remain a fundamental barrier. The community critically lacks a scientific analysis revealing whether VLMs faithfully reach answers or plausibly make guesses. This work aims to provide a fundamental understanding of how VLMs perceive the physical world, and utilize physical laws, while assessing the reliability of model confidence. We propose NICE and FACT, a dual-diagnostic paradigm that explicitly decomposes quantitative reasoning for kinematic physics: FACT diagnoses visual fidelity, physical law comprehension, and temporal grounding. NICE studies our novel neighborhood-informed calibration method and novel metrics to evaluate and calibrate confidence reliability. Evaluated across 6 latest state-of-the-art VLMs, we uncover that models fail to identify visual preconditions or utilize necessary physical laws to reach answers. This work highlights and establishes a standardized diagnostic paradigm to guide the development of faithful, physically-grounded VLMs.

</details>


### [38] [CapCLIP: A Vision-Language Representation Alignment Approach for Wireless Capsule Endoscopy Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08493)
*Haroon Wahab,Irfan Mehmood,Hassan Ugail*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CapCLIP，一个面向无线胶囊内窥镜(WCE)的领域特定视觉-语言表征学习框架。通过将内窥镜帧与基于标准命名法和病理感知模板的临床文本描述对齐，CapCLIP学习语义丰富且可迁移的嵌入。在严格零样本条件下，该框架在KNN分类、CLIP式图文分类和跨模态检索三项下游任务中持续优于现有视觉和视觉-语言基础模型，尤其在分布外数据集上的零样本图文分类和跨模态检索中表现突出。结果表明语言引导的表征学习能提升WCE分析的泛化能力和语义可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有WCE分析方法存在三大局限：1) 主要基于纯视觉方法，局限于狭窄的病理类别；2) 在不同数据集和中心间迁移能力有限；3) 无法有效利用临床文本信息的语义指导。这些限制制约了WCE在临床中的实际应用价值。

Method: CapCLIP采用视觉-语言对比学习框架，核心创新在于：1) 构建基于标准命名法的病理感知文本描述模板；2) 将WCE帧与临床文本描述进行跨模态对齐；3) 学习语义信息丰富且具有跨域可迁移性的联合嵌入空间。

Result: 在严格零样本条件下，CapCLIP在KNN分类、CLIP式图文分类和文本到图像检索三项任务中均优于现有开源视觉和视觉-语言基础模型。特别是在分布外数据集的零样本图文分类和跨模态检索任务中取得显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 语言引导的表征学习能够有效提升WCE分析的泛化能力和语义可解释性，CapCLIP作为面向胶囊内窥镜的专用基础模型，验证了语言引导WCE分析范式的可行性与价值。

Abstract: Wireless capsule endoscopy (WCE) enables non-invasive visual assessment of the small bowel, but its clinical utility is constrained by the large volume of frames generated per examination and the difficulty of recognising subtle abnormalities under highly variable imaging conditions. Existing learning-based approaches for WCE are predominantly vision-only, often confined to narrow pathology sets, and show limited transfer across datasets and centres. To address these limitations, this study introduces CapCLIP, a domain-specific vision-language representation learning framework for WCE. CapCLIP aligns capsule endoscopy frames with clinically grounded textual descriptions derived from standardised nomenclature and pathology-aware caption templates, thereby learning embeddings that are both semantically informed and transferable. The proposed framework is evaluated against relevant open-source vision and vision-language foundation models under strict zero-shot conditions using unseen WCE datasets. Evaluation covers three downstream tasks: K-nearest neighbour classification, CLIP-style image-text classification, and text-to-image retrieval. Across these settings, CapCLIP consistently outperforms the compared baselines, with particularly strong gains in zero-shot image-text classification and cross-modal retrieval on out-of-distribution datasets. The results indicate that language-guided representation learning can improve both generalisation and semantic interpretability in WCE analysis. These findings position CapCLIP as a step toward foundation models tailored to capsule endoscopy and support the use of language-grounded WCE analysis.

</details>


### [39] [Geometric Flood Depth Estimation: Fusing Transformer-Based Segmentation with Digital Elevation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08521)
*Nhut Le,Ehsan Karimi,Maryam Rahnemoonfar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从单目航拍图像估算洪水深度的几何方法，利用Mask2Former模型生成2D洪水分割掩码，再结合数字高程模型(DEM)计算水面高程和逐像素水深，在FloodNet和CRASAR-U-DROIDS数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的2D语义分割只能提供洪水的平面范围，缺乏垂直维度信息，无法评估可通航性和结构风险。而水动力模拟延迟高，不适用于实时场景。因此需要一种既能获取深度信息又具有低延迟的方法。

Method: 采用Mask2Former（基于Transformer的分割模型）生成精确的2D洪水分割掩码；将掩码与DEM融合，识别水陆边界；计算全局水面高程(Z_water)；基于局部静水平衡原理，计算每个像素的洪水深度。

Result: 在FloodNet和CRASAR-U-DROIDS数据集上进行了评估，验证了利用高性能分割从2D图像中提取3D体积数据的可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法可以在无需水动力模拟延迟的情况下，从单目航拍2D图像中提取3D洪水深度信息，为灾后态势感知提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Post-disaster situational awareness relies heavily on understanding both the extent and the volume of floodwaters. While 2D semantic segmentation provides accurate flood masking, it lacks the vertical dimension required to assess navigability and structural risk. This paper presents a geometric "Water Surface Elevation" approach for estimating flood depth from monocular aerial imagery. Our pipeline utilizes Mask2Former, a state-of-the-art transformer-based segmentation model, to generate precise 2D flood masks. These masks are fused with Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) to identify the water-land boundary, calculate a global water surface elevation ($Z_{water}$), and compute per-pixel depth based on the principle of local hydrostatic equilibrium. We evaluate this workflow using the FloodNet and CRASAR-U-DROIDS datasets, demonstrating how high-performance segmentation can be leveraged to extract 3D volumetric data from 2D imagery without the latency of hydrodynamic simulations.

</details>


### [40] [ZAYA1-VL-8B Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08560)
*Hassan Shapourian,Kasra Hejazi,Olabode M. Sule,Beren Millidge*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZAYA1-VL-8B 是一个基于 ZAYA1-8B 语言模型构建的紧凑型混合专家视觉-语言模型，总参数量9.2B，活跃参数1.4B。该模型在图像理解、推理和计数等基准测试中展现出与 Molmo2-4B 和 InternVL3.5-4B 等领先模型竞争的性能，同时超过了 Qwen2.5-VL-3B、PLM-3B 和 MolmoE-1B。其架构创新包括：(1) 将视觉专属 LoRA 适配器集成到 LLM 中，在不增加专家数量的前提下提升模态特定容量；(2) 在 LLM 内部对图像令牌采用双向注意力机制，增强视觉理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持模型紧凑性的同时，提升视觉-语言模型在多模态理解任务上的性能，尤其是在图像理解、推理和计数等基准上达到或超越更大规模模型的水平。

Method: 基于 ZAYA1-8B 语言模型构建 MOE 架构的视觉-语言模型。创新点包括：(1) 在 LLM 中集成视觉专属 LoRA 适配器，在不增加专家数量的前提下增强模态容量；(2) 在 LLM 内部对图像令牌采用双向注意力机制。训练流程涵盖数据组成、序列打包和注意力掩码方案等详细设计。

Result: 模型总参数9.2B，活跃参数1.4B（含视觉编码器）。在图像理解、推理和计数等基准上，性能与 Molmo2-4B 和 InternVL3.5-4B 等领先模型竞争，超越 Qwen2.5-VL-3B、PLM-3B 和 MolmoE-1B。

Conclusion: ZAYA1-VL-8B 证明了紧凑型 MOE 视觉-语言模型可以通过合理的架构创新（LoRA 适配器和双向注意力）实现与更大型模型竞争的性能。

Abstract: We present ZAYA1-VL-8B, a compact mixture-of-experts vision-language model built upon our in-house language model, ZAYA1-8B. Despite its compact size, ZAYA1-VL achieves performance competitive with leading base models such as Molmo2-4B and InternVL3.5-4B, while surpassing models including Qwen2.5-VL-3B, PLM-3B, and MolmoE-1B across a range of image understanding, reasoning, and counting benchmarks. The architecture incorporates two key innovations: (1) vision-specific LoRA adapters integrated into the LLM to increase modality-specific capacity without increasing the number of experts, and (2) bidirectional attention over image tokens within the LLM to enhance visual understanding. We detail the full training pipeline including data composition at each stage, sequence packing, and the attention masking scheme. The model comprises 9.2B total parameters, with 1.4B active parameters including the vision encoder, and is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/Zyphra/ZAYA1-VL.

</details>


### [41] [MicroDiffuse3D: A Foundation Model for 3D Microscopy Imaging Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08566)
*Yongkang Li,Brian Wong,King Wai Chiu,Hanwen Xu,Tangqi Fang,Erin Dunnington,Dan Fu,Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroDiffuse3D，一个预训练的3D显微图像恢复基础模型，能够从高速采集的低分辨率退化测量中重建高质量体积结构，在16倍体积稀疏超分辨率、分辨率和噪声联合退化以及低信噪比去噪三个挑战性场景中均优于强基线方法，显著提升了化学成像的吞吐量和信噪比限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学成像虽能实现无标记可视化并提供传统荧光显微难以获得的生化信息，但3D成像数据采集速度慢，限制了其在术中诊断、药物响应分析等领域的广泛应用。

Method: 提出MicroDiffuse3D，一个预训练的基础模型，用于3D显微图像恢复，从低分辨率退化测量中重建高质量体积结构。

Result: 在16倍体积稀疏超分辨率下，分割质量提升10.58%，线剖面一致性提升15.59%；在三种挑战性恢复场景（3D超分辨率、联合退化、低SNR去噪）中均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 预训练3D恢复策略可有效克服体积化学成像中的通量和信噪比限制，实现以往难以达到的高分辨率分析规模与速度。

Abstract: Chemical imaging enables label-free visualization of cells, tissues and living systems while providing direct biochemical information that is difficult to obtain with conventional fluorescence microscopy. Despite its promise in applications ranging from intraoperative diagnosis to drug-response analysis, its broader use remains limited by slow data acquisition, particularly for three-dimensional imaging. Here we present MicroDiffuse3D, a pretrained foundation model for 3D microscopy image restoration that recovers high-quality volumetric structure from degraded low-resolution measurements acquired at substantially higher throughput. We evaluated MicroDiffuse3D across three challenging restoration settings, including 3D super-resolution under 16-fold volumetric sparsity, joint degradation in resolution and noise, and 3D denoising in the low signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) regime, where the model delivered clear gains over strong baselines. Under the sparse 3D super-resolution setting, MicroDiffuse3D produced clearer continuity across depth with fewer artifacts and improved segmentation quality by 10.58% and line-profile concordance by 15.59%. Together, our results establish pretrained 3D restoration as a broadly applicable strategy for overcoming the throughput and SNR limitations in volumetric chemical imaging, enabling high-resolution analysis at scales and speeds that were previously difficult to achieve.

</details>


### [42] [ACWM-Phys: Investigating Generalized Physical Interaction in Action-Conditioned Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08567)
*Haotian Xue,Yipu Chen,Liqian Ma,Zelin Zhao,Lama Moukheiber,Yuchen Zhu,Yongxin Che*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ACWM-Phys基准，用于评估动作条件世界模型在多种物理动力学（刚体、运动学、可变形物体、粒子系统）下的视频预测能力，并发现模型在分布外泛化时依赖于视觉模式而非真正的物理理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型基准局限于自我中心导航或窄任务机器人数据集，缺乏对多样化物理交互的覆盖，无法全面评估模型对物理世界的泛化理解能力。

Method: 构建了基于可控模拟器的ACWM-Phys基准，包含刚体动力学、运动学、可变形物体交互和粒子动力学四类物理场景，并设计了分布内和分布外测试协议来评估插值和泛化能力。

Result: 在ACWM-DiT模型上的实验表明：OoD泛化能力取决于物理机制和有效任务复杂度；模型在视觉简单、低维交互上泛化良好，但在可变形接触、高维控制和复杂关节运动上性能下降明显；交叉注意力改善高维动作条件化，因果VAE优于帧级编码器，更大的动作空间虽更难建模但能提供更丰富的控制信号。

Conclusion: 模型仍严重依赖视觉外观模式而非完全学习底层物理规律，这些发现为设计物理基础的世界模型提供了指导。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models (ACWMs) have shown strong promise for video prediction and decision-making. However, existing benchmarks are largely restricted to egocentric navigation or narrow, task-specific robotics datasets, offering only limited coverage of the rich physical interactions required for generalized world understanding. We introduce ACWM-Phys, a new benchmark for evaluating action-conditioned prediction under diverse physical dynamics in a clean, controllable simulation environment with a carefully designed action space. ACWM-Phys contains training and evaluation data spanning rigid-body dynamics, kinematics, deformable-object interactions, and particle dynamics. To evaluate both interpolation and generalization, we design in-distribution and out-of-distribution protocols with controlled shifts in interaction patterns or scene configurations. By building the benchmark in a fully controllable simulator, ACWM-Phys enables precise data collection, reproducible evaluation, and systematic analysis of model capabilities for physically grounded world modeling. Through systematic experiments on ACWM-DiT, we find that OoD generalization depends not only on the physical regime but also on effective task complexity: models generalize well on visually simple, low-dimensional interactions with clear geometric structure, but suffer larger drops on deformable contacts, high-dimensional control, and complex articulated motion. This suggests that the model still relies heavily on visual appearance patterns instead of fully learning the underlying physics. Ablations show that cross-attention improves high-dimensional action conditioning, causal VAEs outperform frame-wise encoders, and larger action spaces are harder to model but can improve generalization by providing richer control signals. These findings guide the design of physically grounded world models.

</details>


### [43] [Enhancing Consistency Models for Multi-Agent Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08572)
*Alen Mrdovic,Qingze,Liu,Danrui Li,Mathew Schwartz,Kaidong Hu,Sejong Yoon,Mubbasir Kapadia,Vladimir Pavlovic*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ECTraj提出了一种增强的一致性模型（CM）管线，通过改进的学生-教师训练范式（教师显式融合预测与真实轨迹）和利用CM直接去噪实现多轨迹生成，在Argoverse 2数据集上实现了单步高质量轨迹预测，显著降低推理延迟并提升预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型因迭代去噪导致推理延迟高，难以用于自动驾驶等时间敏感场景。现有快速采样变体（如DDIM、初始噪声分布优化）要么无法实现真正的单步生成，要么受限于噪声分布选择。一致性模型（CM）可实现高质量单步生成但难以从头训练。

Method: 提出ECTraj管线，扩展学生-教师一致性训练：教师将模型预测与部分真实轨迹显式融合，提供更强监督信号；利用CM的直接去噪能力在训练中实现Top-K多轨迹生成；结合条件生成与增强的一致性目标。

Result: 在Argoverse 2大规模数据集上取得竞争性新基准，实现更快推理速度和更高预测精度。

Conclusion: 通过增强CM训练范式和条件生成，ECTraj有效克服了CM难训练和扩散模型推理慢的双重问题，为多智能体轨迹预测提供了高效的单步生成方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models for multi-agent trajectory prediction are limited by iterative denoising, which causes inference latency that hinders their use in time-critical settings like autonomous driving. Fast-sampling variants using DDIM and informed initial noise distributions partially alleviate this issue, but they either fail to achieve true single-step generation or are constrained by the chosen noise distribution. Consistency Models (CMs) offer high-quality one-step generation by mapping noise directly to data, but are difficult to train from scratch . We propose ECTraj, an enhanced CM pipeline with improved training and conditional generation for trajectory prediction. Our framework extends the student-teacher consistency training scheme: the student produces standard outputs, while the teacher explicitly fuses its predictions with parts of the ground truth to give stronger supervision. We also exploit CMs' direct denoising for top-K multi-shot generation during training. Combining conditional generation with this enhanced consistency objective yields faster inference and improved prediction accuracy, establishing competitive new benchmarks on the large-scale Argoverse 2 dataset.

</details>


### [44] [Post-hoc Selective Classification for Reliable Synthetic Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08574)
*Kaixiang Zheng,Jacob H. Seidman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对合成图像检测器在协变量偏移下不可靠的问题，提出ReSIDe框架，通过将logit概念推广到中间层、引入偏好优化算法聚合多层置信度分数，实现后验选择性分类，在不重新训练的情况下最高降低69.55%的AURC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像检测器在分布内表现良好，但面对常见的协变量偏移（covariate shifts）时可靠性不足，检测精度大幅下降。为避免因错误预测带来的风险，需要采用选择性分类策略，让检测器在低置信度时选择弃权。

Method: 提出ReSIDe框架：1) 从质心匹配（centroid matching）视角将logit概念推广到检测器的中间层，使基于logit的置信度评分函数可应用于任意层；2) 引入偏好优化算法，通过最小化风险-覆盖率曲线下面积（AURC）的上界来聚合多层置信度分数，得到最终置信度估计。

Result: 实验表明，ReSIDe显著提升了多种基于logit的置信度评分函数在常见协变量偏移下的选择性分类性能，最高可实现69.55%的AURC降低。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效解决了传统logit置信度函数在协变量偏移下的病态行为，为合成图像检测提供了可靠的后验选择性分类方案。

Abstract: As synthetic images become increasingly realistic, reliable synthetic image detection techniques are of pressing need to prevent their misuse. Despite satisfactory in-distribution performance, deep neural network-based synthetic image detectors (SIDs) lack reliability in deployment and often fail in the presence of common covariate shifts, resulting in poor detection accuracy. To avoid the risk caused by potential errors, we adopt a selective classification (SC) strategy by allowing SIDs to abstain from making low confidence predictions. For practicality, we focus on post-hoc methods which perform confidence estimation on a given SID without retraining. However, we show that conventional logit-based confidence score functions (CSFs) exhibit pathological behavior under covariate shifts, leading to SC performance close to or even worse than random guessing. To address this, we propose a simple yet effective SC framework for Reliable Synthetic Image Detection (ReSIDe). First, we generalize the notion of logits to an SID's intermediate layers from a centroid matching perspective, extending the use of logit-based CSFs to any layer of an SID. Then, we introduce a preference optimization algorithm that aggregates confidence scores extracted from different layers to a final confidence estimate by minimizing an upper bound of the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC). Extensive experimental results show that ReSIDe significantly boosts the SC performance of various logit-based CSFs under common covariate shifts, achieving up to 69.55% AURC reduction.

</details>


### [45] [Improving Generative Adversarial Networks with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08577)
*Antoni Nowinowski,Krzysztof Krawiec*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SD-GAN通过将EMA生成器作为教师模型，利用感知损失指导活跃生成器（学生）进行自蒸馏训练，从而提升GAN的图像质量和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GAN中EMA生成器仅用于最终部署而不参与训练过程，这浪费了其潜在的教学价值。作者希望利用EMA生成器作为教师来指导训练中的生成器。

Method: 将EMA生成器作为教师，活跃生成器作为学生，通过感知损失（perceptual loss）进行知识蒸馏。在Dirac-GAN设定下证明了局部渐近稳定性，并分析了其对寄生循环行为的抑制作用。

Result: 在多个架构和数据集上，SD-GAN显著提升了FID和随机FID指标，稳定了优化轨迹，且提供的学习指导与传统对抗损失非平凡相关。对预训练GAN模型的微调同样有效。

Conclusion: SD-GAN是一种简单有效的自蒸馏框架，在不引入额外计算开销的前提下，同时提升了生成质量和训练稳定性，并具有理论保证。

Abstract: In modern GANs, maintaining an Exponential Moving Average (EMA) of the generator's weights is a standard practice, as such an averaged model consistently outperforms the actively trained generator. However, the EMA generator is used for final deployment only and does not influence the training process. To address this missed opportunity, we introduce Self-Distilled GAN (SD-GAN) that employs the EMA generator as a teacher to guide the active generator (student) via perceptual loss. We prove the local asymptotic stability of SD-GAN in the Dirac-GAN setting and show that it dampens the parasitic cycling behavior that plagues the conventional GANs. Empirical evaluations across established architectures and datasets demonstrate that SD-GAN improves the final image quality on several metrics (FID and random-FID in particular), stabilizes the optimization trajectory and provides additional learning guidance that is not trivially correlated with the conventional adversarial loss. It also proves effective for fine-tuning pretrained GAN models.

</details>


### [46] [PromptDx: Differentiable Prompt Tuning for Multimodal In-Context Alzheimer's Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08585)
*Lujia Zhong,Yihao Xia,Shuo Huang,Jianwei Zhang,Yonggang Shi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出PromptDx框架，通过可微提示调优机制将预训练的TabPFN（上下文学习引擎）与多模态医学数据（3D MRI和表格生物标志物）无缝集成，在阿尔茨海默病诊断中仅用1%的上下文样本即超越传统参数化方法，实现更高效、更符合临床诊断范式的诊断-by-参照框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在医学影像中作为参数化记忆运行，与临床医生通过类比推理参考过往病例的诊断方式存在巨大差距；现有上下文学习框架（如TabPFN）针对表格数据设计，依赖不可微的预处理流程，导致在处理异构多模态数据时出现流形不匹配和梯度断裂问题。

Method: 提出PromptDx框架，核心包含两个组件：(1) 可微提示调优（DPT）机制，通过训练轻量级适配器作为不可微预处理器（如符号编码、独热编码等）的可微替代，实现端到端优化；(2) 掩码多模态建模模块，将3D MRI和表格生物标志物对齐到预训练TabPFN的输入空间，从而在ICL范式下实现多模态提示的联合优化。

Result: 在ADNI数据集上，PromptDx优于传统参数化基线；仅用1%的上下文样本即可达到标准ICL使用30%样本的性能，展现了卓越的流形压缩能力；在六个不同规模的表格数据集上也验证了DPT框架的泛化性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种数据效率更高且更符合临床实践的新范式——诊断-by-参照框架，为阿尔茨海默病诊断提供了更具临床对齐性的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep learning models in medical imaging typically operate as parametric memory, diagnosing patients by recalling fixed knowledge learned during training. This contrasts sharply with clinical practice, where physicians employ analogical reasoning to diagnose new cases by referencing similar records from past exemplars. While In-Context Learning (ICL) frameworks such as Tabular Prior-Fitted Networks (TabPFN) offer a promising diagnosis-by-reference paradigm, they are designed with tabular-specific inductive priors and rely on non-differentiable preprocessing pipelines, leading to manifold mismatch and gradient fracture when applied to heterogeneous multimodal data. To address these limitations, we propose PromptDx, a novel diagnosis-by-reference framework that leverages a pre-trained TabPFN as an ICL engine while enabling seamless integration with multimodal representations. Our core contribution is a Differentiable Prompt Tuning (DPT) mechanism that aligns a Masked Multimodal Modeling module with the pre-trained ICL engine. By training a lightweight adapter as a differentiable surrogate for the engine's non-differentiable preprocessors, we enable an end-to-end optimization of multimodal prompts within the ICL paradigm. We validate our method on the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative (ADNI) dataset using 3D MRI and tabular biomarkers. Experiments demonstrate that our approach outperforms traditional parametric baselines. Notably, our method achieves superior performance using only 1% context samples compared to 30% in standard ICL, demonstrating exceptional manifold condensation ability. We further validate the generalizability of our DPT framework across six tabular datasets with diverse scales. Overall, our method offers a more data-efficient and clinically aligned paradigm for Alzheimer's Disease diagnosis.

</details>


### [47] [S2FT: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning in Sparse Spectrum Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08589)
*Baoquan Zhang,Zhehao Yu,Lisai Zhang,Kenghong Lin,Tianran Chen,Yuxi Sun,Yunming Ye,Yao He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现PEFT中权重变化的频谱并非稀疏而是均匀分布，提出S2FT方法：通过可逆变换将具有稀疏谱的潜在空间域矩阵映射为权重变化，利用重排操作将预估计的粗权重变化平滑化，仅用0.08%训练参数即取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于傅里叶变换的PEFT方法假设权重变化δW具有稀疏频谱，但作者观察到实际频谱呈功率均匀分布，仅微调少量频谱系数无法准确建模均匀频谱的权重变化。

Method: 提出S2FT框架：1) 预估计粗权重变化作为先验；2) 寻找可逆变换（行列重排操作）将潜在稀疏谱空间域矩阵映射到实际权重变化，使空间结构平滑同时保留神经元结构信息；3) 通过最近邻搜索求解重排问题；4) 在稀疏谱域上进行少量频谱系数的微调。

Result: 仅使用0.08%训练参数即取得优于现有方法的性能，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: S2FT通过可逆变换解决了权重变化频谱均匀分布的问题，实现了极低参数下的高效微调。

Abstract: Parameter Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) is a key technique for adapting a large pretrained model to downstream tasks by fine-tuning only a small number of parameters. Recent methods based on Fourier transforms have further reduced the fine-tuned parameters scale by only fine-tuning a few spectral coefficients. Its basic assumption is that the weight change δW is a spatial-domain matrix with a sparse spectrum. However, in this paper, we observe that the spectrum of weight change is not sparse, but instead distributed like power-uniform. This fact implies that fine-tuning only a few spectral coefficients is insufficient to accurately model the weight change with uniform spectrum. To address this issue, we propose to seek an invertible transformation that can transform a latent spatial-domain matrix with sparse spectrum to the weight change, and then perform PEFT on such sparse spectrum domain with few spectral coefficients, called S2FT. To seek such transformation, we first pre-estimate a coarse weight change as a prior. Then, inspired by that sparse spectrum often correspond to locally smooth spatial structures, we regard this transformation as a row and column rearrangement operation on the pre-estimated weight change that smooth spatial structures while keep the structure information of neurons. Finally, we propose to solve the rearrangement search problem in a simple nearest neighbor search manner, thereby obtaining the invertible transformation. Extensive results show our S2FT achieves superior performance by only using 0.08% training parameters.

</details>


### [48] [Beyond Toy Benchmarks: A Systematic Evaluation of OOD Detection Methods For Plant Pathology Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08618)
*Devesh Shah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了六种OOD检测方法在Plant Pathology 2021数据集上的表现，发现基于能量的微调方法在保持分布内准确率的同时，在OOD检测上表现最佳，并揭示了实际部署中的训练不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法大多在小型、视觉同质的基准上评估，缺乏在真实世界细粒度任务上的验证，该工作旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Plant Pathology 2021数据集上比较了六种OOD检测方法，包括事后评分、辅助目标、基于能量的模型和约束优化，并进行嵌入空间与评分函数的分析。

Result: 基于能量的微调在OOD检测中表现最优，同时保持分布内准确率；约束优化方法在扩展到中等规模数据集时出现训练不稳定性。

Conclusion: 在真实世界领域特定数据上可以实现原则性的OOD检测，基准评估可能无法完全捕捉实际部署中的挑战。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for reliable deployment of deep learning systems, yet the majority of existing methods are evaluated on small, visually homogeneous benchmarks. In this work, we study six OOD detection methods spanning post-hoc scoring, auxiliary objectives, energy-based models, and constrained optimization on the Plant Pathology 2021 dataset, a fine-grained task with natural distribution shifts. Energy-based fine-tuning performs best across OOD settings, improving detection over the softmax baseline while preserving in-distribution accuracy. Analysis shows these gains stem from both a restructuring of the embedding space alongside calibration of the scoring function. We further document practical training instabilities that arise when scaling constrained optimization methods to moderate-sized datasets, findings that are largely absent from existing literature. Our results demonstrate that principled OOD detection is achievable on real-world domain-specific data and that benchmark evaluations alone may not capture the challenges that emerge in practice.

</details>


### [49] [Kinematics-Driven Gaussian Shape Deformation for Blurry Monocular Dynamic Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08635)
*Yeon-Ji Song,Kiyoung Kwon,Junoh Lee,Jin-Hwa Kim,Byoung-Tak Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Kinematics-GS提出了一种运动学感知框架，通过将模糊建模为运动对齐变形并引入运动学先验来重参数化高斯形状沿运动轨迹，从而从模糊单目视频中重建动态3D场景，无需辅助运动监督，在真实数据集上显著优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从模糊单目视频中重建动态3D场景极具挑战性，因为运动引起的模糊将物体运动与几何纠缠在一起，阻碍了几何一致性。

Method: 将模糊建模为运动对齐变形，引入运动学先验沿运动轨迹重参数化高斯形状以避免退化的形状坍塌；通过时间形变方差将场景分解为动态和静态组件；采用由粗到细的形变策略捕捉全局运动和细粒度细节。

Result: 在具有真实运动模糊的真实世界基准上，Kinematics-GS在单目动态场景重建中显著优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该框架在处理复杂非刚性运动场景方面具有有效性，展现了在模糊单目视频动态重建中的领先性能。

Abstract: Reconstructing dynamic 3D scenes from blurry monocular videos is challenging as motion-induced blur entangles object motion and geometry, hindering geometric consistency. We present Kinematics-GS, a kinematics-aware framework that models blur as motion-aligned deformation and introduces a kinematic prior to reparameterize Gaussian shapes along motion trajectories, thereby mitigating degenerate shape collapse without auxiliary motion supervision. To stabilize optimization, we decompose scenes into dynamic and static components using temporal deformation variance and employ a coarse-to-fine deformation strategy to capture both global motion and fine-grained details. We also introduce a challenging real-world dataset of deformable and elastic objects exhibiting non-rigid motion with spatially non-uniform motion blur that obscures geometric cues. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks with realistic motion blur demonstrate that Kinematics-GS outperforms prior methods by a clear margin in monocular dynamic scene reconstruction, highlighting its effectiveness in handling complex and non-rigid motion scenarios.

</details>


### [50] [FlowADMM: Plug-and-play ADMM with Flow-based Renoise-Denoise Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08640)
*Hendrik Sommerhoff,Michael Moeller*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowADMM，将基于流模型的确定性去噪-重噪算子集成到ADMM框架中，用于解决逆问题，在保证收敛性的同时实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散和流模型的即插即用方法依赖随机重噪-去噪操作，导致收敛行为难以分析。本文旨在形式化确定性的重噪-去噪算子，并提供收敛保证。

Method: 识别并形式化了流模型PnP方法中隐含的确定性重噪-去噪算子（去噪器在潜在噪声分布上的期望），将该算子集成到经典ADMM框架中，提出FlowADMM算法，并在弱Lipschitz条件下建立了收敛性分析。

Result: 在去噪、去模糊、超分辨率和修复等多个逆问题任务上，FlowADMM在基于流的PnP方法中达到了SOTA性能，且所需的数据一致性评估次数少于先前方法。

Conclusion: FlowADMM通过确定性算子视角和ADMM框架的结合，在收敛性理论和实际性能之间取得了良好平衡，为流模型PnP方法提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Plug-and-play (PnP) methods for solving inverse problems have recently achieved strong performance by leveraging denoising priors based on powerful generative diffusion and flow models. However, existing diffusion- and flow-based PnP methods typically rely on stochastic renoise-denoise operations, which complicate the analysis of their convergence behavior. In this work, we identify and formalize the deterministic renoise-denoise operator underlying flow-based plug-and-play methods. This perspective reveals that these methods implicitly define a deterministic operator given by the expectation of a denoiser over the latent noise distribution. Building on this insight, we propose FlowADMM, a PnP algorithm that integrates the renoise-denoise operator into the classical alternating direction method of multiplier (ADMM) framework. We establish convergence guarantees for FlowADMM under weak Lipschitz conditions on the underlying flow network, and extend the analysis to non-stationary time schedules. Empirically, FlowADMM achieves state-of-the-art performance among flow-based PnP methods on a range of inverse problems, including denoising, deblurring, super-resolution, and inpainting, while requiring fewer data consistency evaluations than prior approaches.

</details>


### [51] [Privacy-Aware Video Anomaly Detection through Orthogonal Subspace Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08651)
*Lei Wang,Wenxiang Diao,Andrew Busch,Jun Zhou,Yongsheng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出正交投影层(OPL)和引导式正交投影层(G-OPL)，通过弱监督方式抑制人脸属性等隐私信息，同时保留姿态和运动等非识别特征，实现隐私感知的视频异常检测，在不牺牲检测精度的情况下有效减少敏感信息泄露。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频异常检测系统过分关注检测精度而忽略隐私保护，尤其在涉及人的场景中，模型可能无意中编码人脸等可识别信息，限制了实际部署的可行性。

Method: 提出轻量级正交投影层(OPL)去除任务无关变异；进一步提出引导式OPL(G-OPL)，利用人脸存在信号的弱监督抑制面部属性；通过余弦对齐目标确保面部信息的一致捕获与去除，无需身份标签或对抗训练。

Result: 实验表明，将隐私约束嵌入模型设计可在维持甚至提升检测精度的同时有效减少敏感信息，验证了基于投影架构的隐私感知VAD方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 投影式架构是设计隐私感知视频异常检测的一种原则性方法，能够平衡检测性能与隐私保护需求。

Abstract: Video anomaly detection (VAD) systems often prioritize accuracy while overlooking privacy concerns, limiting their suitability for real-world deployment. We propose the Orthogonal Projection Layer (OPL), a lightweight module that removes task-irrelevant variations to produce representations focused on anomaly-relevant cues. To address privacy risks in human-centered scenarios, we introduce Guided OPL (G-OPL), which suppresses facial attributes using weak supervision from face-presence signals while preserving non-identifying features such as pose and motion. A cosine alignment objective enforces consistent capture and removal of facial information without identity labels or adversarial training. We further present a privacy-aware evaluation framework that jointly assesses detection performance and privacy preservation, and enables analysis of how sensitive information is filtered. Experiments show that embedding privacy constraints into model design reduces sensitive information while maintaining or improving detection accuracy, supporting projection-based architectures as a principled approach for privacy-aware VAD.

</details>


### [52] [CAST: Channel-Aware Spatial Transfer Learning with Pseudo-Image Radar for Sign Language Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08663)
*Md. Shakhoyat Rahman Shujon,Sheikh Md. Galib Mahim,Md. Milon Islam,Md Rezwanul Haque,Md Rabiul Islam,Hamdi Altaheri,Fakhri Karray*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAST是一种双流架构，利用通道感知空间迁移学习，结合物理感知信号表示（CVD+RTM）与预训练视觉骨干（ConvNeXt-Tiny和EfficientNetV2-S），在仅使用60GHz雷达Range-Time Maps的条件下实现孤立手语识别，Top-1准确率达80.5%，较最佳单模型提升3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达手语识别面临两个核心挑战：一是60GHz雷达仅提供幅度信息的Range-Time Maps（RTM），缺乏相位信息，限制了识别精度；二是传统对数值压缩信号进行频谱分析会产生谐波伪影，影响特征质量。因此需要设计物理感知的信号表示方法，在雷达-only约束下提升识别性能。

Method: 提出CAST双流架构，包含三个核心组件：1) 显式的dB-to-linear逆变换结合窗函数FFT提取Cadence Velocity Diagrams（CVD），避免log压缩信号的谐波伪影；2) 交叉天线空间注意力模块（cross-antenna spatial attention），在卷积前对原始天线通道施加注意力，保留接收器间幅度协方差；3) 非对称交叉注意力机制，融合并行ConvNeXt-Tiny（处理CVD）和EfficientNetV2-S（处理RTM）两个预训练视觉骨干的特征。

Result: 在5折交叉验证下，CAST达到80.5%的Top-1准确率，较最佳单模型基线（77.2%）提升3.3%。

Conclusion: 物理感知的信号表示是雷达-only手语识别在有约束传感器模态下的一个 promising 方向，代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose CAST, a dual-stream architecture that utilizes channel-aware spatial transfer learning for isolated sign language recognition addressing the challenges of magnitude-only 60~GHz radar Range-Time Maps (RTM). The proposed framework combines three physics-aware architectures with pretrained vision backbones, which operate under radar-only constraints across clinical and alphabetical gestures. First, an explicit decibel-to-linear inversion is combined with a windowed fast Fourier transform that extracts Cadence Velocity Diagrams (CVD) while avoiding the harmonic artifacts that arise from the spectral analysis of log-compressed signals. Second, a cross-antenna spatial attention module applies attention to raw antenna channels before the convolution, preserving inter-receiver amplitude covariance. Third, an asymmetric cross-attention mechanism fuses representations from parallel ConvNeXt-Tiny (CVD) and EfficientNetV2-S (RTM) backbones. Extensive experiments reveal that the architecture achieves a Top-1 accuracy of 80.5% under 5-fold cross-validation, establishing a 3.3% improvement over the best single-model baseline (77.2%). The findings suggest that physics-aware signal representations form a promising direction for radar-only sign language recognition under constrained sensor modalities. The source code is available at: https://github.com/Shakhoyat/CAST-at-SignEval2026.

</details>


### [53] [IPAD-CLIP: Teaching CLIP to Detect Image Local Perceptual Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08664)
*Juan Wang,Xinyu Sun,Ke Zhang,Jin Wang,Bing Li,Weiming Hu,Liang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出图像感知伪影检测(IPAD)任务，针对局部细微伪影(鬼影、镜头光晕、摩尔纹)检测空白，构建了含3,520张图像(520张真实+3,000张合成)的基准数据集，并提出基于CLIP的IPAD-CLIP框架，通过学习伪影感知文本嵌入引导视觉编码器关注低级伪影，显著优于现有异常检测和篡改检测方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像质量评估方法严重偏向全局失真(如噪声、模糊)，忽略了鬼影、镜头光晕、摩尔纹等局部感知伪影；尽管伪影去除取得了显著进展，但自动伪影检测这一基础问题尚未得到充分探索。

Method: (1) 形式化定义IPAD任务；(2) 构建含3,520张图像的基准数据集(520张真实+3,000张合成)，提供像素级标注；(3) 提出IPAD-CLIP框架，学习伪影感知文本嵌入以显式建模对象-伪影关系，并将其作为锚点引导视觉编码器从高层语义注意力转向低级伪影

Result: IPAD-CLIP在基准测试中显著优于先进的图像异常检测和篡改检测方法，实现了资源高效的CLIP适配检测。

Conclusion: 这是首个从数据集和模型两方面解决多类局部感知伪影检测的研究。

Abstract: Current image quality assessment methods are heavily biased towards global distortions (e.g., noise, blur), neglecting local perceptual artifacts such as ghosting, lens flare, and moire effects. Although significant progress has been made in artifact removal, the fundamental problem of automatic artifact detection remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we formalize the Image Perceptual Artifact Detection (IPAD) task to address this gap. We contribute a benchmark dataset comprising 3,520 artifact images, including 520 real-captured and 3,000 synthetic samples, each paired with pixel-level masks across three representative artifact categories. The core challenge of IPAD lies in the localized, subtle, and semantically weak nature of these artifacts, which makes them prone to missed detection. To overcome this, we introduce IPAD-CLIP, a novel framework built upon CLIP that enhances artifact discrimination in both textual and visual spaces while preserving generalization capabilities. Our key insight is that local artifacts often exhibit strong correlations with specific semantic contexts. Accordingly, we learn artifact-aware text embeddings to explicitly model the object-artifact relationships, resulting in enhanced representations that clear differentiate between clean and artifact prompts. These text embeddings are then used as anchors to shift the visual encoder's attention from high-level semantics to subtle, low-level artifacts. Extensive experiments demonstrate that IPAD-CLIP offers a resource-efficient adaptation of CLIP for detection, significantly outperforming advanced image anomaly detection and manipulation detection methods on our benchmark. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study addressing multi-class local perceptual artifact detection in terms of both dataset and model.

</details>


### [54] [Supersampling Stable Diffusion and More: An Approach for Interpolating Neural Networks Using Common Interpolation Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08698)
*Md Abu Obaida Zishan,Jannatun Noor,Annajiat Alim Rasel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出通过核插值（Kernel Interpolation）方法，在不微调Stable Diffusion模型的情况下，实现高分辨率图像生成，解决了物体重复伪影问题，并证明该方法可推广到全连接层，且能减少至少4倍训练内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stable Diffusion等DDPM-based模型在生成超出训练分辨率的图像时会出现物体重复伪影（object duplication artifacts），现有方法如膨胀卷积（dilated convolution）虽有效但存在零间隔（zero-gapped）导致难以微调的问题，而patch diffusion等方法无法高效解决该问题。

Method: 提出核插值（Kernel Interpolation）方法，对Stable Diffusion模型的卷积核进行插值缩放，并乘以常数系数以正确缩放卷积核。该方法无需任何训练（zero training），可扩展到全连接层，并支持至少4倍的内存压缩。

Result: 在超出训练分辨率的图像生成任务上取得了有竞争力的实证结果；在扩展到高维训练数据的全连接层插值时，准确率和F1分数相对于基线的性能下降最多仅2.6%；训练内存占用可减少至少4倍。

Conclusion: 核插值是一种通用且高效的方法，无需微调即可解决高分辨率生成中的物体重复问题，并可推广到全连接层，同时显著降低训练内存开销。

Abstract: Stable Diffusion (SD) has evolved DDPM (Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model) based image generation significantly by denoising in latent space instead of feature space. This popularized DDPM-based image generation as the cost and compute barrier was significantly lowered. However, these models could only generate fixed-resolution images according to their training configuration. When we attempt to generate higher resolutions, the resulting images show object duplication artifacts consistently. To solve this problem without finetuning SD models, recent works have tried dilating the convolution kernels of the models and have achieved a great level of success. But dilated kernels are harder to fine-tune due to being zero-gapped. Apart from this, other methods, such as patched diffusion, could not solve the object-duplication problem efficiently. Hence, to overcome the limitations of dilated convolutions, we propose kernel interpolation of SD models for higher-resolution image generation. In this work, we show mathematically that interpolation can correctly scale convolution kernels if multiplied by a constant coefficient and achieve competitive empirical results in generating beyond-training-resolution images with Stable Diffusion using zero training. Furthermore, we demonstrate that our method enables interpolation of deep neural networks to adapt to higher-dimensional training data, with a worst-case performance drop of $2.6\%$ in accuracy and F1-Score relative to the baseline. This shows the applicability of our method to be general, where we interpolate fully-connected layers, going beyond convolution layers. We also discuss how we can reduce the memory footprints of training neural networks, using our method up to at least $4\times$.

</details>


### [55] [Gate-and-Merge: Zero-shot Compositional Personalization of Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08702)
*Guodong Ding,Angela Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Gate-and-Merge框架，一种零样本的组合个性化方法。每个概念独立学习为轻量LoRA适配器，推理时通过在权重空间中合并特定概念LoRA更新实现组合，并采用门控机制抑制干扰、选择相关模块，稳定组合效果。在多个个性化任务中均取得一致性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在组合个性化任务中，需要同时识别或描述多个用户定义的概念，但缺乏零样本组合能力，且通常要求概念共现训练数据，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出Gate-and-Merge框架：(1) 每个概念独立学习为轻量LoRA适配器，保持基础模型不变、概念解耦；(2) 推理时在权重空间中合并概念特定LoRA更新实现组合；(3) 采用门控机制估计文本和视觉线索，选择贡献预测的模块，抑制无关激活和干扰；(4) 组合最有意义且相互一致的更新，保持每个概念的身份。

Result: 定量和定性分析表明，在多个个性化任务的单概念和组合设置中均取得一致性性能提升。

Conclusion: Gate-and-Merge框架有效实现了零样本组合个性化，无需共现训练数据，通过解耦学习和门控合并机制解决了概念组合中的干扰问题。

Abstract: This paper tackles compositional personalization of vision-language models (VLMs). In this problem, multiple user-defined concepts must be recognized or described jointly at test time. We introduce Gate-and-Merge, a zero-shot framework that enables compositional personalization without the need for co-occurrence training. During personalization, each concept is learned independently as a lightweight LoRA adapter, paired with a concept token. The base model remains unchanged and concepts are kept disentangled. At inference, we enable composition by merging concept-specific LoRA updates directly in weight space. To suppress irrelevant activations and prevent interference, a gating mechanism is employed to estimate textual and visual cues and select only the modules that contribute to the prediction. We further stabilize composition by combining only the most meaningful and mutually consistent updates, helping preserve each concept's identity. Our quantitative and qualitative analyses show consistent gains in performance across multiple personalization tasks in both single-concept and compositional settings.

</details>


### [56] [UniShield: Unified Face Attack Detection via KG-Informed Multimodal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08709)
*Hongrui Li,Yichen Shi,Hongyang Wang,Yuhao Gao,Hui Ma,Jun Feng,Zitong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniShield提出了一种基于知识图谱的多模态推理框架，用于统一面部攻击检测，通过构建面部攻击知识图谱（FAKG）和图一致推理优化（GCRO），在多个评估协议上实现了优于判别式方法和通用多模态大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一面部攻击检测方法（包括判别式和基于提示的方法）主要依赖外观关联，缺乏基于证据的可解释推理能力，无法在共享决策空间中同时有效识别物理欺骗和数字伪造。

Method: 1) 构建面部攻击知识图谱（FAKG），将攻击类别与诊断性视觉线索及攻击条件关联；2) 合成52,025个FAKG-QA样本进行攻击图指令微调（AGIT）；3) 提出图一致推理优化（GCRO），采用基于GRPO的目标函数和KG一致性奖励，鼓励生成与图谱支持线索匹配的推理过程，惩罚不一致声明。

Result: 在统一面部攻击检测基准上，UniShield在二元、粗粒度、细粒度三种协议下均取得优异成绩，ACC持续高且HTER低，表明结构化攻击知识能同时提升检测精度和推理可靠性。

Conclusion: 结构化攻击知识可以同时提升检测准确性和推理可靠性，效果优于判别式基线和通用多模态大模型。

Abstract: Unified face attack detection (UAD) requires recognizing physical spoofing and digital forgery within a shared decision space, yet existing discriminative or prompt-based methods largely rely on appearance correlations and provide limited evidence-grounded reasoning. We propose UniShield, a knowledge-grounded multimodal reasoning framework for unified face attack defense. UniShield constructs a Face Attack Knowledge Graph (FAKG) that links attack categories to diagnostic visual cues and attack-conditioned relations, and uses it to synthesize 52,025 FAKG-QA examples for Attack-Graph Instruction Tuning (AGIT). To improve rationale consistency, we further introduce Graph-Consistent Reasoning Optimization (GCRO), a GRPO-based objective with a KG-consistency reward that encourages generated rationales to match graph-supported cues while penalizing incompatible claims. Experiments on our multimodal UAD benchmark show that UniShield achieves strong performance across binary, coarse-grained, and fine-grained protocols, with consistently high ACC and low HTER. These results suggest that structured attack knowledge can improve both detection accuracy and reasoning reliability over discriminative baselines and general-purpose MLLMs. Our code will be released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Unishield-A6A3/.

</details>


### [57] [From Articulated Kinematics to Routed Visual Control for Action-Conditioned Surgical Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08712)
*Bohan Li,Shuojue Yang,Baorui Peng,Xianda Guo,Erli Zhang,Youqi Tao,Junfeng Duan,Daguang Xu,Qi Dou,Xin Jin,Wenjun Zeng,Hao Zhao,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器人手术的动作条件视频生成方法，通过运动学到视觉的提升范式将关节运动学转化为五种图像对齐控制模态，并设计分层路由视觉控制框架动态分配条件容量，结合预算训练和推理方案实现高效计算，在构建的新基准上取得了优于多种基线的动作忠实度、视觉保真度和跨域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件的手术视频生成对于机器人手术至关重要且极具挑战性，其核心困难在于低维控制向量必须精确地支配复杂的图像空间演化。现有方法难以有效处理这一问题。

Method: 1) 提出运动学到视觉的提升范式，将关节运动学转化为五种图像对齐控制模态；2) 设计分层路由视觉控制框架，选择性激活最相关的控制模态和运动尺度，而非统一应用所有控制信号；3) 设计运动学先验引导的路由损失函数，确保物理意义、时间稳定性和专家有效利用；4) 提出预算训练和推理方案，利用路由引起的稀疏性丢弃低显著性控制路径，实现自适应计算。

Result: 在构建的新基准上，该方法在动作忠实度、视觉保真度和跨域泛化方面一致优于多种基线方法；高效变体在保持强控制精度的同时大幅降低了延迟。

Conclusion: 分层路由视觉控制框架能有效解决手术视频生成中低维控制到高维视觉的映射难题，预算训练方案在效率和性能间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Action-conditioned surgical video generation is a critical yet highly challenging problem for robotic surgery. The core difficulty is that low-dimensional control vectors must precisely govern complex image-space evolution. In this work, we propose a kinematic-to-visual lifting paradigm that converts articulated kinematics into a unified set of five image-aligned control modalities. Building on this representation, we introduce a hierarchically routed visual control framework that selectively activates the most relevant control modalities and motion scales. Instead of uniformly applying all control signals, our model performs hierarchical routing to dynamically allocate conditioning capacity. We further design kinematic-prior-guided routing loss functions to ensure physically meaningful, temporally stable, and efficient expert utilization. To improve efficiency, we propose a budgeted training and inference scheme that leverages routing-induced sparsity. By selectively discarding low-significance control pathways during training and execution, our approach enables adaptive computation that is complementary to standard distillation. We additionally construct a new benchmark with curated articulated annotations, obtained through human-in-the-loop semantic labeling and differentiable pose tracking, providing realistic supervision for action-conditioned surgical video generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method consistently improves action faithfulness, visual fidelity, and cross-domain generalization over diverse baselines. Moreover, our efficient variant achieves substantial reductions in latency while maintaining strong control accuracy.

</details>


### [58] [EAR: Enhancing Uni-Modal Representations for Weakly Supervised Audio-Visual Video Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08723)
*Huilai Li,Xiaomeng Di,Ying Xing,Yonghao Dang,Yiming Wang,Jianqin Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过增强单模态表示来提升弱监督音视频解析性能的框架，包含基于相似度的标签迁移预训练和软约束单模态特征建模，在伪标签生成和音视频解析任务上均达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有弱监督音视频解析方法过度聚焦多模态融合，忽视了音视频信号本质上的不对齐特性，导致对单模态事件的感知不足，产生噪声伪标签和次优的解析性能。

Method: 1) 相似度标签迁移方法：用于标注预训练数据，使伪标签生成器更好地理解单模态事件；2) 软约束单模态特征建模：与多模态融合并行进行，精细化建模单模态特征。

Result: 在伪标签生成和音视频解析两个任务上均超越现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 同时关注单模态和跨模态表示能有效提升弱监督音视频解析的事件定位性能。

Abstract: Weakly supervised Audio-Visual Video Parsing (AVVP) aims to recognize and temporally localize audio, visual, and audio-visual events in videos using only coarse-grained labels. Faced with the challenging task settings, existing research advances along two main paths: pre-training pseudo-label generators for fine-grained cross-modal semantic guidance, or refining AVVP model architectures to enhance audio-visual fusion. However, since audio and visual signals are typically unaligned, achieving accurate video parsing fundamentally relies on precise perception of uni-modal events. Yet these multi-modal focused strategies excessively emphasize multi-modal fusion while inadequately guiding and preserving uni-modal semantics, resulting in noisy pseudo-labels and sub-optimal video parsing performance. This paper proposes a novel framework that enhances uni-modal representations for both the pseudo-label generator and the AVVP model. Specifically, we introduce a similarity-based label migration approach to annotate pre-training data, thereby enabling the pseudo-label generator to better understand uni-modal events. We also employ a soft-constrained manner to refine modeling of uni-modal features in parallel with multi-modal fusion. These designs enable coordinated attention to both uni-modal and cross-modal representations, thus boosting the localization performance for events. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both pseudo-label and AVVP performance.

</details>


### [59] [SynerMedGen: Synergizing Medical Multimodal Understanding with Generation via Task Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08724)
*Weiren Zhao,Yi Dong,Cheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 SynerMedGen 框架，基于"生成对齐理解"原则，通过任务对齐统一医学图像的理解与生成，在22项医学图像合成任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一医学模型将理解和生成视为互不相干的目标，缺乏有意义的协同作用，亟需探索何种形式的理解才能真正有益于生成。

Method: 提出生成对齐理解原则，设计三种生成对齐理解任务，采用两阶段训练策略（先理解训练再生成训练），将理解学习到的有益表征迁移到医学图像合成中。

Result: 仅通过理解训练即实现22项医学图像合成任务的强零样本性能；结合生成训练后持续超越现有SOTA专用模型和统一模型；发布1M配对的合成样本和2M生成派生理解实例的大规模数据集SynerMed。

Conclusion: 生成对齐的理解任务能够有效协同医学图像的理解与生成，为统一医学建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Unifying multimodal understanding and generation is a compelling frontier that is beginning to emerge in the medical field. However, the limited existing unified medical models typically treat understanding and generation as disjoint objectives, lacking a meaningful functional synergy. In this work, we identify and address a critical question in unified medical modeling: what form of understanding truly benefits generation. We present SynerMedGen, a unified framework built on the proposed principle of generation-aligned understanding, which synergizes understanding objectives with generation tasks via task alignment. SynerMedGen introduces three generation-aligned understanding tasks and a two-stage training strategy that transfers generation-beneficial representations learned during understanding training to medical image synthesis. Remarkably, even with understanding training alone, our SynerMedGen achieves strong zero-shot performance across 22 medical image synthesis tasks and demonstrates robust generalization to unseen datasets. When combined with generation training, SynerMedGen consistently outperforms state-of-the-art specialized medical image synthesis models as well as recent unified medical models. We also release a large-scale dataset named SynerMed consisting of 1M paired synthesis samples and 2M generation-derived understanding instances to support further research on understanding-generation synergy. Our project can be accessed at https://github.com/Mhilab/SynerMedGen.

</details>


### [60] [Unison: Harmonizing Motion, Speech, and Sound for Human-Centric Audio-Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08729)
*Shihao Cheng,Jiaxu Zhang,Quanyue Song,Shansong Liu,Zhizhi Guo,Xiaolei Zhang,Chi Zhang,Xuelong Li,Zhigang Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Unison是一个统一框架，通过语义引导的音频解耦与双向跨模态强制策略，在人类中心视频中实现运动、语音和音效三模态的协同生成，显著提升了音频感知质量与跨模态同步性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频-视频生成模型难以在运动、语音和环境音效之间保持一致的跨模态对齐，常出现模态间不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 音频流内采用语义引导的谐波策略，通过双向音频交叉注意力和语义条件门控解耦语音与音效生成；2) 音频-运动同步采用双向跨模态强制策略，利用更清洁的模态引导更嘈杂的模态，配合渐进稳定策略。

Result: 在音频感知质量和跨模态同步方面均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 显式的多模态谐波在人类中心视频生成中至关重要。

Abstract: Motion, speech, and sound effects are fundamental elements of human-centric videos, yet their heterogeneous temporal characteristics make joint generation highly challenging. Existing audio-video generation models often fail to maintain consistent alignment across these modalities, leading to noticeable mismatches between motion, speech, and environmental sounds. We present Unison, a unified framework that explicitly promotes coherence across the motion, speech, and sound modalities. Within the audio stream, Unison employs a semantic-guided harmonization strategy that decouples the generation of speech and sound-effect components. Leveraging bidirectional audio cross-attention and semantic-conditioned gating for semantic-driven adaptive recomposition, this approach effectively mitigates speech dominance and enhances acoustic clarity. For audio-motion synchronization, we propose a bidirectional cross-modal forcing strategy where the cleaner modality guides the noisier one through decoupled denoising schedules, reinforced by a progressive stabilization strategy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Unison achieves state-of-the-art performance in both audio perceptual quality and cross-modal synchronization, highlighting the importance of explicit multimodal harmonization in human-centric video generation.

</details>


### [61] [CollabVR: Collaborative Video Reasoning with Vision-Language and Video Generation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08735)
*Joowon Kim,Seungho Shin,Joonhyung Park,Eunho Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CollabVR，一种VLM-VGM协同视频推理框架，通过步骤级闭环控制（VLM规划下一步动作→VGM生成片段→VLM检查修复）解决视频生成模型在目标任务中的长期漂移和中间误差累积问题，在多个基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有"Thinking with Video"方法使用视频生成模型进行视觉推理时，存在两个关键失败模式：多步任务中的长期漂移和片段内的模拟误差累积。这是由于缺乏基于VGM短程视觉先验的显式推理，而VLM自然适合承担此角色，但如何放置VLM（前置规划或事后批评）都存在不足。

Method: 提出CollabVR闭环框架，在步骤级粒度耦合VLM与VGM：VLM规划下一步动作→VGM生成对应视频片段→VLM检查生成结果→将验证器诊断直接融入下一个动作提示以修复检测到的失败。三者形成闭环迭代。

Result: 在Gen-ViRe和VBVR-Bench上，CollabVR在同等计算量下优于单次推理、Pass@k及先前的测试时扩展基线，对开源和闭源VGM均有提升，且最难任务上增益最大。在推理微调后的VGM上仍能进一步提升，表明步骤级VLM监督与推理微调正交且可叠加。

Conclusion: 步骤级VLM监督是提升VGM目标导向推理能力的有效范式，与推理微调正交可叠加，为视频生成模型的推理能力增强提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent "Thinking with Video" approaches use Video Generation Models (VGMs) for visual reasoning by producing temporally coherent Chain-of-Frames as reasoning artifacts. Even strong VGMs, however, exhibit two recurring failure modes on goal-directed tasks: long-horizon drift on multi-step tasks and mid-clip simulation errors that compound. Both stem from the absence of explicit reasoning built upon the VGM's short-horizon visual prior, a role naturally filled by Vision-Language Models (VLMs), but where to place the VLM is non-trivial: upfront plans commit before any frame is generated and post-hoc critiques over whole videos intervene too late. We propose VLM-VGM Collaborative Video Reasoning (CollabVR), a closed-loop framework that couples the VLM with the VGM at step-level granularity: the VLM plans the immediate next action, inspects the clip the VGM generates, and folds the verifier's diagnosis directly into the next action prompt to repair detected failures. On Gen-ViRe and VBVR-Bench, CollabVR improves both open-source and closed-source VGMs over single-inference, Pass@$k$, and prior test-time scaling baselines at matched compute, with the largest gains on the hardest tasks. It also yields further improvements on top of a reasoning-fine-tuned VGM, indicating that step-level VLM supervision is orthogonal to and stackable with reasoning-oriented fine-tuning. We provide video samples and additional qualitative results at our project page: https://joow0n-kim.github.io/collabvr-project-page.

</details>


### [62] [ReorgGS: Equivalent Distribution Reorganization for 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08739)
*Luchao Wang,Kaimin Liao,Qian Ren,Hua Wang,Zhi Chen,Yaohua Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对3D高斯泼溅模型优化收敛后仍存在参数化退化（高透明度漂浮物遮挡梯度、冗余重叠聚类导致参数强耦合）的问题，提出ReorgGS方法，通过重采样中心、估计局部各向异性协方差、初始化低透明度来重组分布结构，在保持场景支持的同时改善梯度可达性并减少重叠，从而在相同优化预算下提升拟合质量、抑制漂浮物并降低渲染开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS模型收敛后可能处于参数化退化状态：高透明度漂浮物通过alpha合成削弱真实表面的梯度信号，冗余重叠簇产生近乎共线的雅可比响应，导致参数块强耦合，使得持续优化陷入平台期而无法移除残余伪影。

Method: 将现有高斯集视为经验概率场，从中重采样中心；利用kNN估计局部各向异性协方差；初始化低透明度；继续使用原始3DGS渲染器和损失函数进行优化。不同于仅调整不透明度的opacity reset，ReorgGS重建了中心、协方差和可见性结构以改变计算图。

Result: 在相同额外优化预算下，ReorgGS在固定高斯数量时提升了拟合质量，抑制了持久漂浮物，并减少了因冗余重叠带来的渲染开销。

Conclusion: 分布等价不等同于优化等价；重组的模型在保持场景覆盖的同时，通过改善梯度可达性和降低不透明度加权重叠来削弱局部参数耦合，从而实现更优的后继优化。

Abstract: A converged 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) model may approximate the target scene while remaining poorly parameterized for further optimization. We identify this failure mode as \emph{parameterization degeneration}: high-opacity floaters attenuate gradients to true surfaces through alpha compositing, and redundant overlapping clusters create strongly coupled parameter blocks with nearly collinear Jacobian responses. These effects explain why continued optimization can plateau even when the model still contains removable artifacts. We propose ReorgGS, an equivalent distribution reorganization method for converged 3DGS models. ReorgGS treats the existing Gaussian set as an empirical probability field, resamples centers from it, estimates local anisotropic covariances with kNN, initializes low opacity, and continues optimization with the original 3DGS renderer and loss. Unlike opacity reset, which only rescales opacity on the old overlap graph, ReorgGS rebuilds centers, covariances, and visibility structure, thereby changing the graph itself. Our analysis shows that distributional equivalence is not optimization equivalence. The reorganized model preserves scene support while improving gradient accessibility under alpha compositing and reducing opacity-weighted overlap, thereby weakening local parameter coupling during subsequent optimization. Under the same additional optimization budget, ReorgGS improves fitting quality at a fixed Gaussian count, suppresses persistent floaters, and reduces rendering overhead from redundant overlap.

</details>


### [63] [Contour-Native Bridge Defect Detection and Compact Digital Archiving with Frequency-Supervised Fourier Contours](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08781)
*Jin Liu,Wang Wang,Hongxu Pu,Zhen Cao,Yasong Wang,Hu Wang,Kunming Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出频率监督傅里叶级数检测（FS-FSD），将桥梁缺陷检测结果表示为紧凑、可恢复的傅里叶轮廓描述符，在统一多边形空间协议下优于边界框和栅格掩码，实现更高效存储与几何精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI辅助桥梁缺陷检测多产生边界框或栅格掩码，前者几何粗糙，后者存储、传输和复用成本高，亟需一种紧凑、可恢复、易共享的轮廓级向量表示方法。

Method: 提出频率监督傅里叶级数检测（FS-FSD），直接回归傅里叶轮廓描述符，并在统一的多边形空间协议下评估边界框、掩码和轮廓。

Result: 在3,767张无人机采集的桥梁图像（42,346个缺陷实例）上，FS-FSD在多边形空间精度和matched-TP几何质量上均优于代表性的检测、分割和轮廓基线方法。

Conclusion: 与边界框和栅格掩码相比，傅里叶轮廓记录能以更紧凑、可恢复、可共享的形式保留缺陷边界几何信息，适用于工程审查和下游信息工作流。

Abstract: AI-assisted bridge defect inspection often produces bounding boxes with crude geometry or raster masks that are costly to store, transmit, and reuse. This study investigates how detected defects can be represented as compact, recoverable contour-level vector records in image space. We propose Frequency-Supervised Fourier Series Detection (FS-FSD), which directly regresses Fourier contour descriptors and evaluates boxes, masks, and contours under a unified polygon-space protocol. On 3,767 UAV-collected bridge images with 42,346 defect instances, FS-FSD achieves higher polygon-space accuracy and better matched-TP geometric quality than representative detection, segmentation, and contour baselines. These results show that, compared with bounding boxes and raster masks, Fourier contour records preserve defect-boundary geometry in a more compact, recoverable, and shareable form for engineering review and downstream information workflows. Future work will study the modeling of multi-region, fragmented, and adjacent bridge-defect boundaries and extend the framework toward long-term bridge-defect tracking and lifecycle-oriented management.

</details>


### [64] [simpleposter: a simple baseline for product poster generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08784)
*Benlei Cui,Fangao Zeng,Weitao Jiang,Yuwen Zhai,Haiwen Hong,Longtao Huang,Hui Xue,Wenxiang Shang,Pipei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimplePoster 提出了一种简洁的基于修复（inpainting）的框架，无需外部控制器（如 ControlNet、OCR 编码器），通过全参数微调基础模型抑制主体延伸，并利用零成本的字符级位置编码实现几何感知文本生成，在主体保留率（98.7%）和文本渲染准确性上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有产品海报生成方法依赖于 ControlNet、OCR 编码器等辅助模块，存在架构复杂、计算开销大、文本错误和主体延伸伪影等问题，亟需一种更简洁高效且能忠实保留产品外观并精确控制多行文本布局的方法。

Method: (1) 对基础模型进行全参数微调，以抑制主体延伸（subject extension）现象，优于 ControlNet 方案；(2) 提出零成本的字符级位置编码（character-level position encoding），无需专用布局模块即可实现几何感知的文本生成。

Result: 主体保留率达 98.7%，远高于 SeedEdit 3.0 的 55.2% 和 PosterMaker 的 85.3%，同时文本渲染准确性也有提升。

Conclusion: SimplePoster 通过全参数微调和字符级位置编码两个关键设计，在简化架构的同时实现了更优的产品主体保留和文本渲染效果，证明了简洁方案的有效性。

Abstract: Product poster generation poses distinct challenges beyond general poster design, requiring both faithful preservation of product appearance and precise control over dense, multi-line text layouts. Prior methods typically adopt inpainting frameworks augmented with auxiliary modules such as ControlNet and OCR encoders. However, these approaches introduce architectural complexity and computational overhead while still suffering from text errors and subject extension artifacts. We present SimplePoster, a simple yet effective inpainting-based framework that achieves faithful subject preservation and accurate, position-controllable text rendering without external controllers. Our approach builds on two observations: (1) full-parameter fine-tuning of the base model effectively suppresses subject extension, outperforming ControlNet-based alternatives; and (2) a zero-cost character-level position encoding enables geometry-aware text generation without dedicated layout modules. Experiments show that SimplePoster achieves a $98.7\%$ subject preservation rate, compared to $55.2\%$ for SeedEdit 3.0 and $85.3\%$ for PosterMaker, while also improving text rendering accuracy. Code, models, benchmark and a part of training data will be available at https://github.com/Alibaba-YuFeng/SIMPLEPOSTER

</details>


### [65] [Lost in Volume: The CT-SpatialVQA Benchmark for Evaluating Semantic-Spatial Understanding of 3D Medical Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08787)
*Mashrafi Monon,Umaima Rahman,Asif Hanif,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CT-SpatialVQA基准，评估3D医学视觉语言模型的语义-空间推理能力，发现现有模型在该任务上平均仅34%准确率，远低于临床可用标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D医学视觉语言模型在VQA和报告生成上表现良好，但不确定它们是否真正学习了空间解剖知识，还是依赖语言先验和相关性。缺乏系统的语义-空间推理评估，影响临床可信度。

Method: 构建CT-SpatialVQA基准，包含9077个来自1601份放射学报告和CT体积的QA对，经LLM辅助管道验证（人工共识一致率95%）。要求模型完成解剖定位、侧向性识别、结构比较和3D结构间关系推理。标准化评估协议测试8个现有3D医学VLM。

Result: 8个3D医学VLM在语义-空间推理任务上平均准确率仅34%，常低于随机水平，表明现有模型严重缺乏空间推理能力。

Conclusion: 当前3D医学VLM在语义-空间推理上存在严重缺陷，需要更深入地融合体积证据才能实现可信的临床决策支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D medical vision-language models have enabled joint reasoning over volumetric images and text, showing strong performance in medical visual question-answering (VQA) and report generation. Despite this progress, it remains unclear whether these models learn spatially grounded anatomy from 3D volumes or rely primarily on learned priors and language correlations. This uncertainty stems from the lack of systematic evaluation of semantic-spatial reasoning in volumetric medical VLMs for clinically reliable decision support. To address this gap, we introduce CT-SpatialVQA, a benchmark designed to evaluate semantic-spatial reasoning in 3D CT data. The benchmark comprises 9077 clinically grounded question-answer (QA) pairs derived directly from 1601 radiology reports and CT volumes, which are validated via a robust LLM-assisted pipeline with a 95% human consensus agreement rate. Our dataset requires explicit anatomical localization, laterality awareness, structural comparison, and 3D inter-structure relational reasoning. We also introduce a standardized evaluation protocol and benchmark eight 3D medical VLMs, finding severe degradation on semantic-spatial reasoning tasks, averaging 34% accuracy and often below random, highlighting the need for deeper integration of volumetric evidence for trustworthy clinical use.

</details>


### [66] [PPU-Bench:Real World Benchmark for Personalized Partial Unlearning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08800)
*Jiahui Guang,Zexun Zhan,Zhenlin Xu,Cuiyun Gao,Haiyan Wang,Jing Li,Zhaoquan Gu,Yanchun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PPU-Bench，一个真实世界、无需微调的多模态大模型个性化部分遗忘基准，包含24K样本和三个渐进攻关设置，并发现现有方法在细粒度事实边界控制上的不足，进而提出边界感知优化（BAO）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM遗忘基准依赖合成知识注入或完整主体级删除，无法捕捉需要细粒度事实控制的个性化删除请求。

Method: 构建PPU-Bench基准（24K样本，500个公众人物，三种遗忘设置）；提出Boundary-Aware Optimization (BAO)方法显式建模主体内遗忘-保留边界。

Result: 完全遗忘常压制视觉身份而非事实知识；选择性和个性化遗忘暴露显著的遗忘-保留权衡及主体内事实边界挑战；鲁棒性分析揭示不同遗忘设置下的不同脆弱点；BAO能有效强制执行主体内事实边界。

Conclusion: PPU-Bench揭示了现有MLLM遗忘方法的细粒度控制不足，BAO为个性化部分遗忘提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) may memorize sensitive cross-modal information during pretraining. However, existing MLLM unlearning benchmarks rely on synthetic knowledge injection or complete subject-level deletion, which fail to capture realistic, personalized deletion requests that require fine-grained factual control. In this paper, we introduce PPU-Bench, a real-world and fine-tuning-free benchmark for personalized partial unlearning in MLLMs. PPU-Bench contains 24K multimodal and unimodal samples derived from pre-existing knowledge of 500 public figures under three progressively challenging settings: Complete, Selective, and Personalized unlearning. The benchmark evaluates whether methods can remove target knowledge while preserving non-target facts, model utility, and cross-modal consistency. Extensive experiments show that Complete Unlearning often suppresses visual identity rather than factual knowledge, while Selective and Personalized Unlearning expose significant forget--retain trade-offs and challenges in intra-subject factual boundaries. Robustness analysis under cross-image and prompt-based attacks reveals distinct vulnerabilities across different unlearning settings. Motivated by these findings, we propose Boundary-Aware Optimization (BAO), which explicitly models intra-subject forget-retain boundaries. Experimental results on two representative methods demonstrate that BAO can effectively enforce intra-subject factual boundaries.

</details>


### [67] [CoLVR: Enhancing Exploratory Latent Visual Reasoning via Contrastive Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08802)
*Ziyang Ding,Linjian Meng,Yiming Wu,Yuhan Li,Yuhao Liu,Zhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoLVR，通过对比学习框架增强多模态大模型在隐空间中的视觉推理探索能力，在多个基准上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐视觉推理方法通过硬对齐目标迫使隐表示匹配预定义视觉特征，严重限制了隐推理过程的探索性。

Method: 1）基于角度扰动的隐对比学习目标，扩展语义隐空间，避免过度约束嵌入；2）隐轨迹对比奖励用于强化学习后训练，实现隐视觉推理过程的细粒度优化。

Result: 在VSP上提升5.83%，Jigsaw上提升8.00%，域外基准MMStar上提升3.40%。

Conclusion: CoLVR显著增强了隐表示的探索能力，在多个视觉推理任务上优于现有隐空间模型。

Abstract: Due to the potential for exploratory reasoning of Latent Visual Reasoning, recent works tend to enable MLLMs (Multimodal Large Language Models) to perform visual reasoning by propagating continuous hidden states instead of decoding intermediate steps into discrete tokens. However, existing works typically rely on hard alignment objectives to force latent representations to match predefined visual features, thereby severely limiting the exploratory of latent reasoning process. To address this problem, we propose CoLVR (Contrastive Optimization for Latent Visual Reasoning). To obtain a more exploratory visual reasoning, CoLVR introduces a latent contrastive training framework. Firstly, CoLVR learns diverse and exploratory representations with a latent contrastive objective guided by angle-based perturbation, which expands the semantic latent space and avoids over-constrained embedding. Then, CoLVR employs a latent trajectory contrastive reward for RL (Reinforcement Learning) post-training to enable fine-grained optimization of latent visual reasoning process and thus fostering diverse reasoning behaviors. Experiments demonstrate that CoLVR significantly enhances the exploratory capability of latent representations, achieving average improvements of 5.83% on VSP and 8.00% on Jigsaw, while also outperforming existing latent models on out of domain benchmarks, with a 3.40% gain on MMStar. The data, codes, and models are released at https://github.com/Oscar-dzy/CoLVR.

</details>


### [68] [Curvature-Aware Captioning:Leveraging Geodesic Attention for 3D Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08808)
*Ziyao He,Yingjie Liu,ZhangYangRui,Mingsong Chen,Xuan Tang,Xian Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Curvature-Aware Captioning框架，通过非欧几里得测地线注意力机制（Oblique空间自注意力+Lorentz空间双向测地线交叉注意力）解决稀疏点云密集描述中定位与上下文建模的冲突，在ScanRefer和Nr3D基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有密集描述方法在处理稀疏点云时，欧几里得嵌入空间难以同时保留细粒度局部几何细节和建模指数级增长的全局语义层级，导致定位不准确或场景描述浅层化、不连贯。

Method: 提出Curvature-Aware Captioning框架：1) Oblique空间中的自注意力机制强制维度同质性并建立长程依赖；2) Lorentz空间中的双向测地线交叉注意力建模跨场景实例的分层语义关系；3) 利用Oblique流形与Lorentz双曲面的曲率互补性解决欧几里得-双曲冲突，通过各向同性优化保证特征稳定性。

Result: 在ScanRefer和Nr3D基准上取得SOTA性能，在定位精度和描述丰富性上均有显著提升。

Conclusion: 理论分析验证了Oblique流形与Lorentz双曲面的曲率互补性能够有效解决定位-上下文冲突，非欧几里得几何在3D场景理解中具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Accurate 3D scene description is fundamental to robotic navigation and augmented reality, yet current dense captioning methods face significant limitations in processing sparse point cloud data. %
Existing approaches that apply Euclidean embedding spaces struggle to simultaneously preserve fine-grained local geometric details and model exponentially growing global semantic hierarchies, leading to either inaccurate localization or disjointed, shallow scene descriptions. %
In this work, we propose a novel \textbf{\textsc{Curvature-Aware Captioning}} framework, integrating novel non-Euclidean geodesic attention mechanisms, to resolve the localization-contextualization conflict. %
Specifically, self-attention within Oblique space enforces dimensional homogeneity while establishing long-range dependencies. Bidirectional geodesic cross-attention within Lorentz space models hierarchical semantic relationships across scene instances, enabling simultaneous precision in object localization and coherence in scene descriptions. %
Theoretical analysis confirms that the curvature complementarity between the Oblique manifold and Lorentz hyperboloid resolves the Euclidean-hyperbolic conflict, ensuring feature stability via isotropic optimization while preserving inherent hierarchical relationships. Extensive experiments on ScanRefer and Nr3D benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, with significant gains in both localization accuracy and descriptive richness.

</details>


### [69] [From pre-training to downstream performance: Does domain-specific pre-training make sense?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08819)
*Felix Krones*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了CNN和Transformer在医学影像中的不同预训练策略（监督学习、自监督学习、不同初始化方式和数据模态），发现只有预训练数据与目标模态高度匹配才能显著提升下游性能，自监督学习在某些情境下优于监督方法但效果因场景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在医学影像领域取得显著进展，但预训练策略与下游性能之间的关系尚不明确，亟需系统研究以提升模型的可靠性和有效性。

Method: 系统比较CNN与Transformer架构，考察监督学习、自监督学习、不同初始化方式和数据模态等多种预训练方法，在自然图像、胸部X光、胸部CT和视网膜OCT图像上进行评估，并分析预训练数据与目标模态匹配程度的影响。

Result: 仅当预训练数据与目标模态高度匹配时，才能显著提升下游性能；自监督学习在某些情况下优于监督方法，但其效果因具体上下文而异。

Conclusion: 预训练策略的选择对医学影像深度学习模型的可靠性至关重要，本研究有助于开发更准确、更可靠的诊断工具，最终改善临床患者预后。

Abstract: Deep learning techniques have revolutionised medical imaging, improving diagnostic accuracy and enabling both more accurate and earlier disease detection. However, the relationship between pre-training strategies and downstream performance in medical imaging models requires further exploration. Here, we systematically compare convolutional neural networks and transformers, examining various pre-training approaches, including supervised and self-supervised learning, as well as different initialisations and data modalities. Models are evaluated on natural images, chest X-rays, chest CT and retina OCT images, considering the effects of matching pre-training data with target modalities. Our findings indicate that only pre-training on data closely matching the target modality significantly improves downstream performance. While self-supervised learning can outperform supervised methods, its effectiveness varies with context. The study underscores the importance of pre-training strategies to enhance the reliability and effectiveness of deep learning models in medical imaging. By addressing these key factors, our research aims to contribute to the development of more accurate and dependable diagnostic tools, ultimately improving patient outcomes in clinical settings.

</details>


### [70] [FraudBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for Detecting AI-Generated Fraudulent Refund Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08820)
*Xinyu Yan,Boyang Chen,Jiaming Zhang,Tiantong Wu,Hong Xi Tae,Yichen He,Tiantong Wang,Yachun Mi,Yurong Hao,Yilei Zhao,Lei Xiao,Longtao Huang,Pengjun Xie,Wei Liu,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FraudBench，一个多模态基准数据集，用于检测 AI 生成的欺诈性退款证据，涵盖电商、外卖和旅行服务场景。实验发现当前 MLLM 和专用检测器在识别虚假损坏证据方面表现不佳，揭示了通用 AI 图像检测与可靠退款证据验证之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 AI 生成图像检测基准主要评估单独的图像真伪分类、跨生成器迁移或伪造定位，未涉及以用户主张为条件的欺诈性证据检测，尤其是 AI 生成的退款欺诈这一现实风险。

Method: 构建 FraudBench 基准数据集，包含真实用户评论证据（真实损坏与未损坏图像）以及使用六种最先进图像编辑/生成模型从真实未损坏图像合成的虚假损坏证据。通过 MLLM 辅助过滤和人工标注进行质量控制。在此数据集上评估 MLLM、专用 AI 生成图像检测器和人类参与者。

Result: 当前 MLLM 能识别真实损坏证据，但在大多数虚假损坏子集上检测率（TPR）远低于 50% 基线。专用检测器表现相对较好但在不同生成器间不一致，且对真实损坏样本产生假阳性。

Conclusion: 通用 AI 图像检测与可靠的条件特定退款证据验证之间存在明显差距，亟需专门面向欺诈证据验证的检测方法。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI)-generated images have become increasingly realistic and readily adaptable to concrete real-world claims, creating new challenges for verifying visual evidence. A concrete emerging risk is AI-generated refund fraud, in which manipulated or synthetic images are used to support claims about damaged products, poor delivery conditions, or service-related defects. Existing AI-generated image detection benchmarks mainly evaluate standalone authenticity classification, cross-generator transfer, or forensic localization, leaving claim-conditioned fraudulent evidence detection underexplored. To bridge this gap, we introduce FraudBench, a multimodal benchmark for detecting AI-generated fraudulent refund evidence. FraudBench is constructed from real-world user-review evidence across e-commerce, food delivery, and travel-service scenarios. We curate real evidence images together with their associated review and product metadata, identify genuine damaged and undamaged evidence through MLLM-assisted filtering and human annotation, and synthesize fake-damaged evidence from genuine undamaged reference images using six state-of-the-art image editing and generation models. Using FraudBench, we evaluate MLLMs, specialized AI-generated image detectors, and human participants under the same settings. Experiments show that current MLLMs often recognize real-damaged evidence but fail on many fake-damaged subsets, with fake-damage detection rates (TPR) far below the 50% baseline on most generator subsets. Specialized detectors generally perform better but remain inconsistent across generators and can produce false positives on real-damaged samples, revealing a clear gap between generic AI image detection and reliable claim-conditioned refund-evidence verification.

</details>


### [71] [Rethinking Event-Based Object Dtection through Representation-Level Temporal Aggregation and Model-Level Hypergraph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08825)
*Meisen Wang,Hao Deng,Wei Bao,Ma Yuanxiao,Chengjie Wang,Zhiqiang Tian,Shaoyi Du,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Ev-DTAD框架，通过层次化时间聚合（HTA）紧凑表示和高频超图时间融合（FHTF）推理，在Gen1（+0.8 mAP、1.7×加速）、1Mpx/Gen4（+0.5 mAP、1.6×加速）和eTraM（+3.0 mAP、2.0×加速）数据集上实现了事件检测精度与效率的竞争性平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件相机目标检测方法在表征层面（时间信息间接编码）和模型层面（难以将碎片化事件响应聚合为高阶物体特征）存在局限。

Method: 提出Ev-DTAD统一框架，包含：(1) 层次化时间聚合（HTA）——紧凑的三通道伪RGB表示，显式编码窗口内与窗口间时间信息；(2) 频率感知超图时间融合（FHTF）——通过时间演化建模和高阶关系推理细化多尺度事件特征。

Result: Gen1上+0.8 mAP且1.7×更快，1Mpx/Gen4上+0.5 mAP且1.6×更快，eTraM上+3.0 mAP且2.0×更快，验证了紧凑时间表示与时间-超图特征推理的互补性。

Conclusion: Ev-DTAD实现了事件检测中精度-效率的竞争性权衡，证明紧凑时间表征与高阶时间推理的有效互补。

Abstract: Event cameras provide microsecond-level temporal resolution, low latency, and high dynamic range, offering potential for perception under fast motion and challenging illumination conditions. However, existing Event-based Object Detection (EOD) methods face limitations at both the representation and model levels: prior event representations usually encode temporal information indirectly through redundant structures, while detection models struggle to explicitly aggregate fragmented event responses into coherent high-order object features. To address these limitations, we present Event Dual Temporal-Relational Aggregation Detector (Ev-DTAD), a unified EOD framework that integrates representation-level temporal encoding with model-level temporal-hypergraph reasoning. Specifically, we introduce Hierarchical Temporal Aggregation (HTA), a compact three-channel pseudo-RGB representation that explicitly embeds temporal information across intra- and inter-window events. To further enhance detection under sparse and fragmented event responses, we propose Frequency-aware Hypergraph Temporal Fusion (FHTF), which refines multi-scale event features through temporal evolution modeling and high-order relational reasoning. Extensive experiments on Gen1 (+0.8 mAP and 1.7$\times$ faster), 1Mpx/Gen4 (+0.5 mAP and 1.6$\times$ faster), and eTraM (+3.0 mAP and \textbf{2.0$\times$ faster}) demonstrate that Ev-DTAD achieves a competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-off, validating the complementarity between compact temporal representation and temporal-hypergraph feature reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [Cross-Sample Relational Fusion: Unifying Domain Generalization and Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08839)
*Zhen-Hao Xie,Yan Wang,Hao Sun,Han-Jia Ye,De-Chuan Zhan,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CORF是一个统一框架，通过选择性样本细化、自适应加权和级联蒸馏，同时解决类别增量学习中的灾难性遗忘和域偏移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景（如自动驾驶）中，CIL系统不仅面临灾难性遗忘，还需处理显著的域偏移（如城市道路到乡村环境、晴天到雨天），现有方法未能同时有效解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 利用空间贡献图对训练样本进行选择性细化，突出语义信息区域以增强泛化性；2) 引入预测置信度自适应加权样本，学习域无关表示；3) 提出级联蒸馏框架，跨多个特征层级捕获样本间关系依赖，实现多粒度知识迁移。

Result: CORF可无缝集成到现有CIL算法中，在多个基准数据集上取得竞争性性能，有效提升泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过统一框架同时处理域偏移和灾难性遗忘，CORF为CIL在复杂现实场景中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) requires a learning system to learn new classes while retaining previously learned knowledge. However, in real-world scenarios such as autonomous driving, a system trained on urban roads in sunny weather may later need to operate in rural or highway environments with different traffic patterns and weather conditions. This requires the model not only to overcome catastrophic forgetting, but also to effectively handle domain shifts. In this paper, we propose CrOss-sample Relational Fusion (CORF), a unified framework to address domain shift and catastrophic forgetting simultaneously.
