We further discover a declarative-procedural asymmetry: representations are far more compatible between prose and mathematics than between either and code, suggesting that the critical axis of divergence is not linguistic vs. formal but declarative vs. procedural.

</details>


### [147] [Hidden Error Awareness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning: The Signal Is Diagnostic, Not Causal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09502)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Julian Su,Haiyue Zhang,Yi Nian,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现CoT推理中模型内部能检测到自己的推理错误，但外部表达却充满自信。通过线性探针可从隐藏状态以0.95 AUROC预测正确性，而口头自信度几乎无差异。这种隐藏的错误感知在多个模型系列中一致存在，但无法用于修复错误——四种干预手段均失败，证明该信号是诊断性的而非因果性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑CoT假设（即生成的推理反映模型的内部计算），通过实验证明模型在隐藏状态中能察觉自身错误但表面自信度无差异，揭示推理过程中内部表征与外部表达的解耦现象。

Method: 使用线性探针从模型各层隐藏状态预测轨迹正确性（AUROC达0.95）；对比文本表面特征分类器（仅0.59）；测试四种干预方法（激活引导、探针引导的best-of-N、自我修正、激活补丁）以检验信号的因果性。

Result: 线性探针从第一步即达到0.79 AUROC，全程0.95；口头自信度错误轨迹4.55/5 vs 正确4.87/5（几乎无差）；文本分类器仅0.59；信号跨Qwen/Llama/Phi系列及1.5B-72B规模一致，DeepSeek-R1达0.852 AUROC；四种干预全部失败，激活补丁破坏输出连贯性。

Conclusion: 隐藏的错误感知信号是诊断性的而非因果性的——它反映计算质量，但无法作为杠杆来引导模型修正错误。这界定了机械可解释性的边界：推理过程中的错误表征与先前成功编辑的事实知识表征有本质不同。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting assumes that generated reasoning reflects a model's internal computation. We show this assumption is wrong in a specific, measurable way: models internally detect their own reasoning errors but outwardly express confidence in them. A linear probe on hidden states predicts trace correctness with 0.95 AUROC -- from the very first reasoning step (0.79) -- while verbalized confidence for wrong traces is 4.55/5, nearly identical to correct ones (4.87/5). A text-surface classifier achieves only 0.59 on the same data, confirming a 0.20-point gap invisible in the generated text. This hidden error awareness holds across three model families (Qwen, Llama, Phi), 1.5B-72B parameters, and RL-trained reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, 0.852 AUROC). The natural question is whether this signal can fix the errors it detects. It cannot. Four interventions -- activation steering, probe-guided best-of-N, self-correction, and activation patching -- all fail; patching destroys output coherence entirely. The signal is diagnostic, not causal: a readout of computation quality, not a lever to redirect it. This delineates a boundary for mechanistic interpretability: error representations during reasoning are fundamentally different from the factual knowledge representations that prior work has successfully edited.

</details>


### [148] [Assessment of RAG and Fine-Tuning for Industrial Question-Answering-Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09533)
*Jakob Sturm,Josef Pichlmeier,Christian Bernhard,Maka Karalashvili,Johannes Klepsch,Georg Groh,Andre Luckow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在汽车行业领域特定问答系统中，RAG和微调两种知识适配方法在回答质量和运营成本上的权衡，发现RAG是兼顾效果和成本效益的最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在企业问答系统中，LLM需要适配领域知识，但RAG和微调两种主流方法在成本-准确率权衡上的优劣尚不明确，缺乏针对工业场景的系统性比较。

Method: 在两个汽车行业封闭数据集上，对比RAG和微调的效果；扩展了Erol等人提出的Cost-of-Pass框架，联合评估输出质量、生成成本和用户交互成本。

Result: 付费模型开箱即用表现最佳；开源模型结合RAG可达到与付费模型相当的质量；总体而言RAG是效果和成本效率最优的适配方法。

Conclusion: 对于汽车领域的企业QA系统，RAG无论是用于闭源还是开源模型，都是最有效且最具成本效益的知识适配方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed in enterprise question-answering (QA) systems, requiring adaptation to domain-specific knowledge. Among the most prevalent methods for incorporating such knowledge are Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and fine-tuning (FT). Yet, from a cost-accuracy trade-off perspective, it remains unclear which approach best suits industry scenarios. This study examines the impact of RAG and FT on two closed datasets specific to the automotive industry, assessing answer quality and operational costs. We extend the Cost-of-Pass framework proposed by Erol et al. (arXiv:2504.13359) to jointly assess output quality, generation cost, and user interaction cost. Our findings reveal that while premium models perform best out of the box, open-source models can achieve comparable quality when enhanced with RAG. Overall, RAG emerges as the most effective and cost-efficient adaptation method for both closed- and open-source models.

</details>


### [149] [TAD: Temporal-Aware Trajectory Self-Distillation for Fast and Accurate Diffusion LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09536)
*Haoyang Zhou,Li Kong,Shijie Ren,Xiting Wang,Shuang Liang,Guowei Wang,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TAD提出了一种时序感知的自蒸馏框架，通过将解码轨迹中的掩码位置按时间分为近端（用硬交叉熵损失）和远端（用软KL散度损失）两类进行训练，有效平衡了扩散大语言模型在并行文本生成中的准确性与速度。实验表明，该方法的Quality模型将平均准确率从46.2%提升至51.6%，Speed模型将平均AUP从46.2提升至257.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型在并行文本生成中面临准确性与并行度之间的权衡问题——增加每次前向传播的token数（TPF）往往会降低生成质量，而现有的加速方法通常以牺牲准确性为代价来换取速度。

Method: 提出TAD（Temporal-Aware trajectory self-Distillation）框架：1）数据构建阶段，让教师模型在提示和真实回复条件下生成解码轨迹，记录中间掩码状态；2）根据剩余解码步数将掩码位置分为近端子集和远端子集；3）对近端token使用基于教师轨迹token的硬交叉熵损失训练学生模型，鼓励对即将解码的token进行自信预测；4）对远端token使用教师与学生token分布之间的软KL散度损失，提供更软的监督并保留未来规划知识；5）由此衍生出两种部署配置：优先准确率的Quality模型和优先加速的Speed模型。

Result: 在LLaDA上，Quality模型将平均准确率从46.2%提升至51.6%，Speed模型将平均AUP从46.2提升至257.1。

Conclusion: TAD框架通过时序感知的蒸馏策略，在扩散大语言模型上持续改善了准确性与并行度之间的权衡，验证了基于时间信息的差异化监督能有效提升并行文本生成的性能。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising paradigm for parallel text generation, but in practice they face an accuracy-parallelism trade-off, where increasing tokens per forward (TPF) often degrades generation quality. Existing acceleration methods often gain speed at the cost of accuracy. To address this limitation, we propose TAD, a Temporal-Aware trajectory self-Distillation framework. During data construction, we condition a teacher model on both the prompt and the ground-truth response to generate decoding trajectories, recording the intermediate masked states throughout the process. Based on how many decoding steps remain before each masked token is revealed, we partition masked positions into near and distant subsets. For near tokens, we train the student with a hard cross-entropy loss using the teacher trajectory tokens as labels, encouraging confident predictions for tokens that are about to be decoded. For distant tokens, we apply a soft KL divergence loss between the teacher and student token distributions, providing softer supervision and preserving future planning knowledge. This temporal-aware partition naturally gives rise to two deployment configurations: a Quality model that prioritizes accuracy and a Speed model that favors more aggressive acceleration. Experiments show that TAD consistently improves the accuracy-parallelism trade-off. On LLaDA, it raises average accuracy from 46.2\% to 51.6\% with the Quality model and average AUP from 46.2 to 257.1 with the Speed model. Our code is available at: https://github.com/BHmingyang/TAD

</details>


### [150] [TacoMAS: Test-Time Co-Evolution of Topology and Capability in LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09539)
*Chen Xu,Yicheng Hu,Ruizi Wang,Xinyu Lin,Wenjie Wang,Dongrui Liu,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TacoMAS提出一种测试时双时间尺度协同进化框架，在推理过程中快速更新智能体能力、慢速调整通信拓扑，实现动态多智能体系统的稳定适应，在四个基准上平均超越最强基线13.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在测试时要么固定拓扑，要么仅单方面调整拓扑或能力，缺乏对两者联合适应及其时间尺度差异的考虑，难以在动态任务中实现有效进化。

Method: 将MAS推理建模为在线图适应任务：快速能力循环利用轨迹级反馈更新智能体专业性；慢速拓扑循环由元LLM驱动，执行边编辑、智能体添加和移除等生灭操作。快慢双环设计驱动MAS向任务条件化的稳定均衡进化。

Result: 在四个基准上对比近20种多智能体基线，平均提升13.3%。

Conclusion: 测试时联合适应能力与拓扑但采用不同时间尺度是提升动态MAS性能的关键，快慢双环设计能有效引导MAS收敛至任务条件化的稳定均衡。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) have emerged as a promising paradigm for solving complex tasks. Recent work has explored self-evolving MAS that automatically optimize agent capabilities or communication topologies. However, existing methods either learn a topology that remains fixed at inference time or adapt only the topology or capability during inference. We empirically and theoretically show that effective test-time evolution requires jointly adapting both axes, but on different time scales: capabilities should update rapidly to handle emerging subtasks, while the topology should evolve more slowly to preserve coordination stability. We then introduce TacoMAS, a test-time co-evolution framework for dynamic MAS. TacoMAS formulates MAS inference as a task of online graph adaptation, where nodes represent agents with role-specific capabilities and edges define their communication topology. During inference, a fast capability loop updates agent expertise using trajectory-level feedback, while a slow meta-LLM-driven topology loop performs agents' birth-death operations on MAS, including edge edit, agent addition, and agent removal. We further show that this fast-slow design drives MAS evolution toward a task-conditioned stable equilibrium. Experiments on four benchmarks demonstrate that TacoMAS outperforms nearly 20 multi-agent baselines, achieving an average improvement of 13.3% over the strongest baseline. The codes are released at https://github.com/chenxu2-gif/TacoMAS-MultiAgent.

</details>


### [151] [Crosslingual On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09548)
*Yihong Liu,Raoyuan Zhao,Michael A. Hedderich,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出跨语言同策略自蒸馏（COPSD）方法，通过在低资源语言任务中引入高资源语言（英语）的翻译和参考解答作为特权上下文，并基于学生模型自身 rollout 进行全分布 token 级蒸馏训练，显著提升了 17 种低资源非洲语言的数学推理能力，优于 GRPO，并改善了格式遵循、测试时扩展和多语言泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在数学推理上取得了显著进展，但这一能力在不同语言之间并不均衡，尤其是低资源语言的推理性能远低于高资源语言。现有方法缺乏有效利用模型自身高资源推理行为来提升低资源语言推理的手段。

Method: COPSD 采用同一模型同时作为学生和教师：学生仅输入低资源语言问题，教师则获得跨语言特权上下文（包括问题的英语翻译和英语参考解答）。训练目标是最小化学生自身 rollout 上 token 级别的全分布散度，提供密集监督，避免结果-only 强化学习（如 GRPO）的稀疏性和不稳定性。

Result: 在 17 种低资源非洲语言上，COPSD 在不同模型规模下均一致提升了低资源数学推理能力，大幅优于 GRPO。此外，COPSD 改善了答案格式遵循度，增强了测试时扩展能力，并在更难的多语言推理基准上表现出良好的泛化性，尤其对更低资源的语言收益更大。

Conclusion: COPSD 是一种有效的跨语言推理能力迁移方法，通过自蒸馏框架将高资源语言的推理行为迁移至低资源语言，避免了传统 RL 方法的稀疏反馈问题，并在多语言低资源场景下取得了显著且一致的提升。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning, but this ability is not equally accessible across languages. Especially low-resource languages exhibit much lower reasoning performance. To address this, we propose Crosslingual On-Policy Self-Distillation (COPSD), which transfers a model's own high-resource reasoning behavior to low-resource languages. COPSD uses the same model as student and teacher: the student sees only the low-resource problem, while the teacher receives privileged crosslingual context, including the problem translation and reference solution in English. Training minimizes full-distribution token-level divergence on the student's own rollouts, providing dense supervision while avoiding the sparsity and instability of outcome-only reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments on 17 low-resource African languages show that COPSD consistently improves low-resource mathematical reasoning across model sizes and substantially outperforms Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Further analyses show that COPSD improves answer-format adherence, strengthens test-time scaling, and generalizes to harder multilingual reasoning benchmarks, with especially large gains for lower-resource languages. We make our code and data available at: https://github.com/cisnlp/COPSD.

</details>


### [152] [Towards Compact Sign Language Translation: Frame Rate and Model Size Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09554)
*Kuanwei Chen,Mengfeng Tsai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的77M参数手语翻译流水线，结合MMPose骨架姿态提取和T5-small模型，通过调整输入帧率在效率和性能间取得平衡，在How2Sign数据集上以12fps实现75%的自注意力计算复杂度降低，BLEU-4仅从10.06降至9.53，且模型比T5-base系统小约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无标注的手语翻译方法依赖大型编码器-解码器模型，限制了实际部署能力，亟需一种更紧凑高效的解决方案。

Method: 采用MMPose提取骨架姿态特征，通过单一线性投影映射到T5-small模型，并通过调整输入帧率（如12fps）来缩短序列长度，降低编码器二次自注意力计算复杂度。

Result: 在How2Sign数据集上，12fps下序列长度减半，自注意力计算复杂度降低75%，BLEU-4为9.53（24fps为10.06）；模型参数量77M，约为T5-base系统的1/3。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构无需层次化编码器或大规模模型即可保持竞争力，证明了紧凑流水线在手语翻译中的可行性与部署潜力。

Abstract: Sign Language Translation (SLT) converts sign language videos into spoken-language text, bridging communication between Deaf and hearing communities. Current gloss-free approaches rely on large encoder-decoder models, limiting deployment. We propose a compact 77M-parameter pipeline that couples MMPose skeletal pose extraction with a single linear projection into T5-small. By varying the input frame rate, we expose a practical efficiency trade-off: at 12 fps the model halves its sequence length, achieving a 75% reduction in encoder quadratic self-attention computational complexity while incurring only a modest BLEU-4 drop (9.53 vs. 10.06 at 24 fps on How2Sign). Our system is roughly 3x smaller than prior T5-base systems, demonstrating that a lightweight architecture can remain competitive without hierarchical encoders or large-scale models.

</details>


### [153] [CLR-voyance: Reinforcing Open-Ended Reasoning for Inpatient Clinical Decision Support with Outcome-Aware Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09584)
*Aishik Nagar,Arun-Kumar Kaliya-Perumal,Yu-Hsuan Han,Andrew Sheng-Han Huang,Kristen Kee,Yushi Cao,Yiming Chen,Hongchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CLR-voyance框架，将住院临床推理建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），利用基于结果且经临床验证的奖励信号进行后训练与评估，在CLR-POMDP基准上达到84.91%，超越GPT-5等前沿模型，并在合作公立医院部署超6个月。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床-大语言模型评估和强化学习奖励信号存在三大问题：将序贯决策坍缩为封闭式检索、泄露临床旅程信息、使用无锚点的LLM-as-judge评分。缺乏将住院推理视为部分可观测序贯决策的框架。

Method: 1) 将住院推理形式化为POMDP，划分为策略可见的过去和仅预言机可见的未来；2) 利用预言机LLM基于过去信息生成病例特定的查询-答案对及首个可验证的自适应评估准则；3) 使用GRPO对Qwen3-8B和MedGemma-4B进行后训练，并进行模型合并。

Result: CLR-voyance-8B在CLR-POMDP上达到84.91%，超越GPT-5（77.83%）和MedGemma-27B（66.66%）；在现有医学基准上表现相当或更优；在大规模临床医生对齐研究中表现优异；已在实际公立医院部署6个月以上。

Conclusion: 将临床推理建模为POMDP并配合结果锚定与临床验证的奖励信号，能有效提升模型在住院场景下的推理能力，同时保持通用能力。该框架还为临床LLM-as-judge和偏好模型选择提供了实证见解。

Abstract: Inpatient clinical reasoning is a sequential decision under partial observability: the clinician sees the admission so far and must choose the next action whose downstream consequences are not yet visible. Existing clinical-LLM evaluations and RL rewards signals collapse this into closed-form retrieval, clinical journey leakage, or unanchored LLM-as-judge scoring. We introduce CLR-voyance, a framework that reformulates inpatient reasoning as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) and supervises it with rewards that are simultaneously outcome-grounded and clinician-validated. We instantiate the formulation as CLR-POMDP, which partitions successful patient journeys into a policy-visible past and an oracle-only future. Using the past information, an oracle LLM generates a case-specific query-answer pair, and the first adaptive rubric for clinical reasoning which is verifiable in the future of the patient journey. These rubrics are used for both post-training and evaluation of models for inpatient clinical reasoning. We post-train Qwen3-8B and MedGemma-4B with GRPO followed by model merging, yielding state-of-the-art inpatient clinical reasoning while retaining generalist capabilities. CLR-voyance-8B achieves 84.91% on CLR-POMDP, ahead of frontier medical reasoning models like GPT-5 (77.83%) and MedGemma-27B (66.66%) and has comparable or better performance on existing medical benchmarks. To ensure a clinically meaningful setting, we conduct a large-scale clinician alignment study, where physicians curate per-case rubrics, grade candidate responses, and provide blinded pairwise preferences of model reasoning. This study provides insights on clinical LLM-as-a-judge and clinical preference-model selection, which can inform the community at large. CLR-voyance has been deployed for 6+ months at a partner public hospital, drafting thousands of reasoning-heavy inpatient notes.

</details>


### [154] [Byte-Exact Deduplication in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Three-Regime Empirical Analysis Across Public Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09611)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证分析了RAG管道中字节级精确分块去重技术，在三种不同冗余场景下可实现0.16%到80.34%的上下文缩减，通过跨厂商5评审员校准评估和人工去噪协议验证，证明该技术在不牺牲模型质量的前提下实现了显著的推理计算节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索RAG管道中字节级精确分块去重是否能在不降低模型生成质量的前提下，通过减少冗余上下文来实现推理计算的节省。

Method: 在三种操作场景（干净学术检索、构造的企业模式、多轮对话AI）中测量上下文缩减率；采用跨5家厂商的5评审员校准面板评估，结合五类人工去噪协议处理多数投票标记为"实质性不同"（MAT）的配对样本，并使用Wilson 95%置信区间上限评估质量退化。

Result: 干净学术检索场景字节缩减0.16%，企业模式缩减24.03%，多轮对话AI缩减80.34%。经人工审计后，所有四个厂商API在干净和高冗余RAG场景下均严格低于5%的MAT阈值，去重引入零可测质量回归。

Conclusion: 字节级精确去重可以在确定性方式下实现显著的推理计算节省，同时不损害评估级别的模型质量。

Abstract: This preprint presents an empirical analysis of byte-exact chunk-level deduplication in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines. We measure context reduction across three distinct operating regimes: clean academic retrieval (0.16% byte reduction on 22.2M BeIR passages), constructed enterprise patterns (24.03% reduction), and multi-turn conversational AI (80.34% reduction). To validate quality preservation, we conducted a cross-vendor 5-judge calibrated panel evaluation across four production APIs (Google Gemini 2.5 Flash, Anthropic Claude Sonnet 4.6, Meta Llama 3.3 70B, and OpenAI GPT-5.1). Applying a five-category human-in-the-loop noise-removal protocol to panel-majority materially different (MAT) pairs, we establish that byte-exact deduplication introduces zero measurable quality regression. Post-audit, all four vendors clear the strict <5% Wilson 95% upper-bound MAT threshold in both the clean and high-redundancy RAG regimes. This work demonstrates that substantial inference compute savings can be achieved deterministically without compromising evaluation-grade model quality.

</details>


### [155] [Scratchpad Patching: Decoupling Compute from Patch Size in Byte-Level Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09630)
*Lin Zheng,Vasilisa Bashlovkina,Timothy Dozat,Dan Garrette,Laura Rimell,Joshua Maynez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scratchpad Patching (SP)方法，通过在字节块内插入临时暂存区（scratchpad）来聚合已观察到的字节信息并刷新块级上下文，从而解决无分词器语言模型中补丁大小与建模质量之间的权衡问题。实验表明，即使在16字节/块的设置下，SP增强模型也能接近字节级基线性能，同时将KV缓存缩小16倍，推理计算量减少3-4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无分词器语言模型（尤其是基于补丁的变体）面临补丁大小与建模质量之间的权衡：更大的补丁可减少计算量和KV缓存占用，但会降低建模质量。作者将此归因于"补丁滞后"（patch lag）现象——在补丁完全被观察到之前，其中的字节预测必须依赖前一个补丁的过时表示以保持因果性，且滞后随补丁增大而加剧。

Method: 提出Scratchpad Patching (SP)，在每个补丁内部插入临时暂存区（scratchpad），用于聚合当前已观察到的字节信息并刷新补丁级上下文，供后续预测使用。SP根据下一个字节预测的熵（信息密度）决定是否触发scratchpad，从而选择性分配计算资源，支持推理时计算量的后验调整。

Result: 在自然语言和代码任务上，SP在相同补丁大小下提升了模型质量。例如，在16字节/补丁的设置下，SP增强模型匹配或接近字节级基线的下游评估性能，同时KV缓存缩小16倍，推理计算量减少3-4倍。

Conclusion: Scratchpad Patching有效缓解了补丁滞后问题，打破了补丁大小与建模质量之间的固有权衡，实现了在保持高质量的同时显著降低计算和存储开销。

Abstract: Tokenizer-free language models eliminate the tokenizer step of the language modeling pipeline by operating directly on bytes; patch-based variants further aggregate contiguous byte spans into patches for efficiency. However, the average patch size chosen at the model design stage governs a tight trade-off: larger patches reduce compute and KV-cache footprint, but degrade modeling quality. We trace this trade-off to patch lag: until a patch is fully observed, byte predictions within it must rely on a stale representation from the previous patch to preserve causality; this lag widens as patches grow larger. We introduce Scratchpad Patching (SP), which inserts transient scratchpads inside each patch to aggregate the bytes seen so far and refresh patch-level context for subsequent predictions. SP triggers scratchpads using next-byte prediction entropy, selectively allocating compute to information-dense regions and enabling post-hoc adjustment of inference-time compute. Across experiments on natural language and code, SP improves model quality at the same patch size; for example, even at $16$ bytes per patch, SP-augmented models match or closely approach the byte-level baseline on downstream evaluations while using a $16\times$ smaller KV cache over patches and $3$-$4\times$ less inference compute.

</details>


### [156] [Can We Trust LLMs for Mental Health Screening? Consistency, ASR Robustness, and Evidence Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09634)
*Erfan Loweimi,Sofia de la Fuente Garcia,Samira Loveymi,Hadi Daneshvar,Saturnino Luz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了三种LLM（Phi-4、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B）在零样本条件下从语音中估计HADS评分的可靠性，重点关注模型内一致性、ASR鲁棒性和证据忠实性三个维度。Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B表现出优异性能（ICC>0.89，关键词基础度>93%），而Llama-3.1-8B在ASR扰动下ICC从0.82骤降至0.36，且存在评分-证据分离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM虽能零样本从语音估计HADS评分，但临床部署需要确保其在模型内一致性、ASR鲁棒性和证据忠实性三个维度的可靠性，现有研究对此缺乏系统评估。

Method: 在111名英语被试上，使用三种LLM（Phi-4、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B）、三种Whisper ASR变体（Large/Medium/Small）和真实转录文本，每个模型-条件组合进行三次独立运行，评估ICC、ASR退化程度、预测效度和关键词基础度。

Result: （i）Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B模型内一致性优秀（ICC>0.89），ASR退化极小；（ii）Llama-3.1-8B在10% WER下ICC从0.82降至0.36；（iii）鲁棒模型的预测效度在ASR下基本保持；（iv）Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B关键词基础度>93%，Llama-3.1-8B仅77-81%；模型间关键词一致性远低于评分一致性，存在评分-证据分离。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在语音心理健康评估的临床可靠性上存在显著差异，评分级一致性不代表解释级一致性，临床可解释性面临挑战。

Abstract: LLMs can estimate Hospital Anxiety and Depression Scale (HADS) scores from speech in a zero-shot manner, but clinical deployment requires reliability across three dimensions: intra-model consistency, ASR robustness, and evidence faithfulness. We evaluate three LLMs (Phi-4, Gemma-2-9B, and Llama-3.1-8B) on 111 English-speaking participants using ground-truth transcripts and three Whisper ASR variants (Large, Medium, Small), with three independent runs per model-condition pair. We find that (i) Phi-4 and Gemma-2-9B achieve excellent intra-model consistency (ICC > 0.89) with minimal degradation under ASR; (ii) Llama-3.1-8B shows ASR-fragile consistency, with ICC dropping from 0.82 to 0.36 at 10% WER; (iii) predictive validity is largely preserved under ASR for robust models; and (iv) keyword groundedness exceeds 93% for Phi-4 and Gemma-2-9B but falls to 77-81% for Llama-3.1-8B. Inter-model keyword agreement is far lower than score-level agreement, revealing a score-evidence dissociation with implications for clinical interpretability.

</details>


### [157] [K12-KGraph: A Curriculum-Aligned Knowledge Graph for Benchmarking and Training Educational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09635)
*Hao Liang,Qihan Lin,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Zhen Hao Wong,Meiyi Qiang,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为弥补现有LLM教育基准仅考查事实回忆的不足，本文构建了基于人教版教材的K12-KGraph课程知识图谱，包含7种节点和9种关系类型；在此基础上创建了K12-Bench多选基准（23,640题，5类图谱任务）和K12-Train训练语料（约2,300 QA对）。实验表明，当前最强模型Gemini-3-Flash在K12-Bench上仅57%精确匹配，而基于K12-Train的SFT在样本效率上显著优于同等规模的通用指令微调语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM教育评估基准（如C-Eval、CMMLU、GaokaoBench、EduEval）主要依赖考试式问答评估事实记忆，缺乏对课程认知（curriculum cognition）的评估——即理解知识间的先修关系、概念分类、实验与概念关联以及教学顺序安排。

Method: (1) 从人教版官方教材中抽取K12-KGraph知识图谱，涵盖数学、物理、化学、生物四学科，含7种节点类型（概念、技能、实验、练习、节、章、书）和9种关系类型（分类、先修、关联、验证、评估、定位、顺序）；(2) 基于图谱构建K12-Bench（23,640道多选题，5类任务：Ground、Prereq、Neighbor、Evidence、Locate）；(3) 基于图结构和节点属性合成K12-Train监督微调语料（约2,300 QA对）。

Result: 在K12-Bench上，Gemini-3-Flash仅达57%精确匹配，最佳开源模型Gemma-4-31B-IT为46%。在严格匹配2,300样本SFT预算下，K12-Train在Qwen3-4B-Base和Llama-3.1-8B-Base上均一致优于同等规模的8个主流指令微调语料子集，在GaokaoBench和EduEval上表现更优。

Conclusion: 课程结构化监督在教育场景微调中具有很高的样本效率，当前LLM在课程认知方面存在显著不足，需要专门的知识图谱引导训练来弥补这一缺陷。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in K-12 education, yet existing benchmarks such as C-Eval, CMMLU, GaokaoBench, and EduEval mainly evaluate factual recall through exam-style question answering. Effective educational AI additionally requires curriculum cognition: understanding how knowledge is structured through prerequisite chains, concept taxonomies, experiment-concept links, and pedagogical sequencing. To address this gap, we introduce K12-KGraph, a curriculum-aligned knowledge graph extracted from official People's Education Press textbooks across mathematics, physics, chemistry, and biology from primary to high school. The graph contains seven node types (Concept, Skill, Experiment, Exercise, Section, Chapter, Book) and nine relation types covering taxonomy, prerequisite, association, verification, assessment, location, and order. Based on this graph, we construct two resources: (1) K12-Bench, a 23,640-question multi-select benchmark spanning five graph-derived task families (Ground, Prereq, Neighbor, Evidence, and Locate); and (2) K12-Train, a KG-guided supervised fine-tuning corpus of approximately 2,300 QA pairs synthesized from graph structure and node attributes. Experiments reveal substantial deficiencies in curriculum cognition: on K12-Bench, Gemini-3-Flash achieves only 57% exact match, while the best open-source model, Gemma-4-31B-IT, reaches 46%. Under a strictly matched 2,300-sample SFT budget on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B-Base, K12-Train consistently outperforms equally sized subsets from eight mainstream instruction-tuning corpora on both GaokaoBench and EduEval, demonstrating that curriculum-structured supervision is highly sample-efficient for educational tuning. We release the graph, benchmark, training data, and full construction pipeline.

</details>


### [158] [MedMeta: A Benchmark for LLMs in Synthesizing Meta-Analysis Conclusion from Medical Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09661)
*Huy Hoang Ha,Benoit Favre,Francois Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MedMeta基准，评估LLM从医学meta分析中合成证据的能力。通过Golden-RAG和Parametric-only两种工作流测试发现：信息检索增强（RAG）显著优于纯参数依赖，领域微调收益有限，且所有模型均无法识别否定性证据。即使在理想RAG条件下，模型得分仅略高于平均水平（~2.7/5.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基准主要测试LLM的事实性记忆能力，而缺乏对其从多个来源进行高阶推理（如证据合成）能力的评估。为了填补这一空白，作者构建了专门评估LLM基于医学meta分析摘要生成结论能力的基准。

Method: 从PubMed收集81篇meta分析（2018-2025年），设计两种评估工作流：1) Golden-RAG：提供真实摘要进行检索增强生成；2) Parametric-only：仅依赖模型内部知识。采用LLM-as-a-judge协议进行评估，并通过Pearson's r（0.81）和Bland-Altman分析验证其与人类专家评分高度一致。

Result: 1) Golden-RAG显著优于Parametric-only；2) 领域微调收益微弱，在提供外部材料时几乎无效；3) 所有模型均无法识别和拒绝否定性证据，暴露RAG系统关键弱点；4) 理想RAG条件下模型得分仅~2.7/5.0。

Conclusion: MedMeta为证据合成提供了具有挑战性的新基准。研究表明，对于临床应用，开发稳健的RAG系统比单独进行模型专业化更有前景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have saturated standard medical benchmarks that test factual recall, yet their ability to perform higher-order reasoning, such as synthesizing evidence from multiple sources, remains critically under-explored. To address this gap, we introduce MedMeta, the first benchmark designed to evaluate an LLM's ability to generate conclusions from medical meta-analyses using only the abstracts of cited studies. MedMeta comprises 81 meta-analyses from PubMed (2018--2025) and evaluates models using two distinct workflows: a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Golden-RAG) setting with ground-truth abstracts, and a Parametric-only approach relying on internal knowledge. Our evaluation framework is validated by a well-structured analysis showing our LLM-as-a-judge protocol strongly aligns with human expert ratings, as evidenced by high Pearson's r correlation (0.81) and Bland-Altman analysis revealing negligible systematic bias, establishing it as a reliable proxy for scalable evaluation. Our findings underscore the critical importance of information grounding: the Golden-RAG workflow consistently and significantly outperforms the Parametric-only approach across models. In contrast, the benefits of domain-specific fine-tuning are marginal and largely neutralized when external material is provided. Furthermore, stress tests show that all models, regardless of architecture, fail to identify and reject negated evidence, highlighting a critical vulnerability in current RAG systems. Notably, even under ideal RAG conditions, current LLMs achieve only slightly above-average performance (~2.7/5.0). MedMeta provides a challenging new benchmark for evidence synthesis and demonstrates that for clinical applications, developing robust RAG systems is a more promising direction than model specialization alone.

</details>


### [159] [The Silent Vote: Improving Zero-Shot LLM Reliability by Aggregating Semantic Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09739)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Semantic Softmax方法，通过聚合目标标签语义邻域的得分，解决大语言模型在零样本分类中因标准softmax丢弃同义词概率质量导致的再归一化偏差（Renormalization Bias）和过置信问题，在Qwen-3和Phi-4-mini模型上显著降低了ECE和Brier Score，提升了AUROC和Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准约束解码在零样本分类中存在Renormalization Bias：当模型被限制在小规模目标标签集时，标准softmax丢弃了原始分布中语义同义词的概率质量（称为Silent Vote），导致人为的过置信和较差的校准性能。

Method: 提出Semantic Softmax，一种推理时层（inference-time layer），通过聚合每个目标标签周围语义邻域的得分来恢复被丢弃的信息。

Result: 在Qwen-3和Phi-4-mini模型上，使用GoEmotions和Civil Comments数据集进行评估，Semantic Softmax在所有指标上一致改进：显著降低Expected Calibration Error (ECE)和Brier Score，同时提升AUROC和Macro-F1。

Conclusion: 通过考虑语言细微差别，该方法为零样本分类提供了更校准、更准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used as zero-shot classifiers in complex reasoning tasks. However, standard constrained decoding suffers from a phenomenon we define as Renormalization Bias. When a model is restricted to a small set of target labels, the standard softmax operation discards the probability mass assigned to semantic synonyms in the original distribution. This loss of information, which we call the Silent Vote, results in artificial overconfidence and poor calibration.
  We propose Semantic Softmax, an inference-time layer that recovers this lost information by aggregating the scores of the semantic neighborhood surrounding each target label. We evaluate this approach on Qwen-3 and Phi-4-mini models using GoEmotions and Civil Comments datasets. Our results demonstrate consistent improvements across all evaluation metrics: Semantic Softmax substantially reduces Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score, while simultaneously enhancing discriminative performance in terms of AUROC and Macro-F1. By accounting for linguistic nuances, our method provides a more calibrated and accurate alternative for zero-shot classification.

</details>


### [160] [Language Models Without a Trainable Input Embedding Table: Learning from Fixed Minimal Binary Token Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09751)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了语言模型中可训练的输入嵌入表是否必要。通过将标准的可训练V×d_model输入嵌入矩阵替换为固定的最小二进制令牌编码（V=65536时K=16）以及零参数提升到模型宽度的方法，在32层decoder-only模型上训练约170亿tokens后，固定编码达到了与可训练基线相当的验证困惑度（2.36 vs 2.44），同时减少了6710万个可训练输入参数。另一种无表仿射重编码变体也表现接近（2.39）。结果表明在该实验条件下，可训练的输入嵌入表并非语言建模的必要组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中可训练的输入嵌入表是否真的必要，试图去除这一大量参数组件以简化模型并提升效率。

Method: 将标准可训练的V×d_model输入嵌入矩阵替换为固定最小二进制令牌编码（V=65536时K=16位），通过平铺（tiling）将16维二进制编码扩展到d_model=1024维度。同时评估了另一种无表变体：从令牌ID动态生成编码，并经过F₂^K上的可逆仿射变换进行随机重编码。输出投影仍保持标准可训练形式。

Result: 在32层decoder-only模型、约170亿tokens训练、三个独立训练种子的实验中，固定最小编码达到验证困惑度2.36（基线2.44），减少了6710万可训练输入参数。无表仿射重编码变体达到2.39。但固定编码与基线之间的差距在种子间4.8%的波动范围内，因此未达到统计显著性的优越性声明。

Conclusion: 在该实验条件下（32层decoder-only模型、约170亿tokens训练），可训练的输入嵌入表对于有效的语言建模并非必要。输出投影仍需保持可训练。

Abstract: Trainable input embedding tables are a standard component of modern language models. We ask whether they are actually necessary at the input interface. For a vocabulary of size $V$, exact token identity requires only $K=\lceil \log_2 V\rceil$ bits. We replace the usual trainable $V\times d_{\text{model}}$ input embedding matrix with fixed minimal binary token codes and a zero-parameter lift to model width. In our main setting, $V=65{,}536$, so $K=16$, and tokens are represented by fixed 16-dimensional binary codes tiled to $d_{\text{model}}=1024$. We also evaluate a fully table-free variant in which codes are generated from token IDs on the fly and randomly recoded by an invertible affine transform over $\mathbb{F}_2^K$. Across matched 32-layer decoder-only models trained on approximately 17B tokens and evaluated over three independent training seeds, fixed minimal codes achieve comparable held-out validation perplexity to a standard learned-input baseline while removing 67.1M trainable input parameters. The fixed-code runs have a lower mean validation perplexity in our experiments, 2.36 versus 2.44, but the observed gap is within the measured seed-to-seed variation of 4.8\%; we therefore interpret the result as evidence that the trainable input table is not necessary, rather than as a statistically resolved superiority claim. The table-free affine-recoded variant remains close at 2.39 despite a slightly shorter training run. These results show that, in this regime, a trainable input embedding table is not necessary for useful language modeling. The output projection remains standard and trainable.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [161] [Where Reliability Lives in Vision-Language Models: A Mechanistic Study of Attention, Hidden States, and Causal Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08200)
*Logan Mann,Ajit Saravanan,Ishan Dave,Shikhar Shiromani,Saadullah Ismail,Yi Xia,Emily Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过统一机制探测管线VRP，系统检验了VLM中“注意力越锐利则模型越可信”的直觉假设，发现注意力结构与正确性几乎无关（相关系数接近0），而隐藏状态几何、层间边界形成和稀疏后期电路才是更可靠的可靠性指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验视觉-语言模型（VLM）中广泛存在的直觉假设：注意力图的锐利程度（集中于查询区域）与模型置信度和校准性正相关。该假设若成立，注意力图可作为无需标注的可信度信号，但直接、系统的验证尚属空白。

Method: 开发统一的机制探测管线VRP，在三个开源VLM系列（LLaVA-1.5、PaliGemma、Qwen2-VL，3-7B参数）上系统比较注意力结构、生成动态、隐藏状态几何与正确性标签之间的关系。采用线性探测、因果消融、自一致性分析等方法。

Result: (1) 注意力结构与正确性近乎零相关（R_pb≈0.001），尽管注意力对特征提取因果必要（top-30%补丁掩码使准确率下降8.2-11.3pp）。 (2) 隐藏状态线性探测在POPE上AUROC>0.95，K=10自一致性是最强行为预测器（R_pb=0.43）。 (3) 因果消融显示架构差异：LLaVA（晚期融合）可靠性集中在脆弱的后期瓶颈，PaliGemma和Qwen2-VL（早期融合）可靠性分布广泛。

Conclusion: 在3-7B VLM中，可靠性更可靠地读取自隐藏状态几何、层间边界形成和稀疏后期电路，而非注意力图锐利程度。该发现对VLM可信度监测具有直接的设计启示。

Abstract: A pervasive intuition holds that vision-language models (VLMs) are most trustworthy when their attention maps look sharp: concentrated attention on the queried region should imply a confident, calibrated answer. We test this Attention-Confidence Assumption directly. We instrument three open-weight VLM families (LLaVA-1.5, PaliGemma, Qwen2-VL; 3-7B parameters) with a unified mechanistic pipeline -- the VLM Reliability Probe (VRP) -- that compares attention structure, generation dynamics, and hidden-state geometry against a single correctness label. Three results emerge. (i) Attention structure is a near-zero predictor of correctness (R_pb(C_k,y)=0.001, 95% CI [-0.034,0.036]; R_pb(H_s,y)=-0.012, [-0.047,0.024] on a pooled n=3,090 split), even though attention remains causally necessary for feature extraction (top-30% patch masking drops accuracy by 8.2-11.3 pp, p<0.001). (ii) Reliability becomes legible later in the computation: a single hidden-state linear probe reaches AUROC>0.95 on POPE for two of three families, and self-consistency at K=10 is the strongest behavioral predictor we measure at 10x inference cost (R_pb=0.43). (iii) Causal neuron-level ablations expose a sharp architectural split with direct monitor-design implications: late-fusion LLaVA concentrates reliability in a fragile late bottleneck (-8.3 pp object-identification accuracy after top-5 probe-neuron ablation), whereas early-fusion PaliGemma and Qwen2-VL distribute it widely and absorb destruction of ~50% of their peak-layer hidden dimension with <=1 pp degradation. The takeaway is narrow but consequential: in 3-7B VLMs, reliability is read more reliably off hidden-state geometry, layer-wise margin formation, and sparse late-layer circuits than off attention-map sharpness.

</details>


### [162] [Embeddings for Preferences, Not Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08360)
*Carter Blair,Ariel D. Procaccia,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出现有文本嵌入在集体决策中无法准确捕捉参与者偏好，原因在于其同时编码了偏好相关信号（立场、价值观）和语义干扰（风格、措辞），这两者在观测上存在相关性。作者通过合成训练数据打破这种相关性，从而将最优评分器从干扰主导的余弦相似度转向偏好主导的度量，在11个在线讨论数据集中显著提升了偏好预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI使参与者以自由文本而非固定选项表达观点成为可能。自然想法是将文本嵌入到向量空间以应用设施定位和公平聚类方法，但标准文本嵌入测量的是语义相似性，而设施定位问题需要的是"偏好相似性"——参与者与文本的距离应与其同意程度成反比。现成的嵌入通过语义与偏好相似性之间的相关性只能获得粗略的偏好信号，当这种相关性失效时便无法捕捉真实偏好。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为一个不变性问题：文本嵌入模型同时编码了偏好相关信号（立场和价值观）和语义干扰（风格和措辞），两者在观测上相关。通过设计合成训练数据打破这种相关性，使最优评分器从依赖于干扰的余弦相似度转向偏好主导的度量。

Result: 在11个在线讨论数据集上，该方法显著提升了偏好预测性能。

Conclusion: 打破语义相似性与偏好相似性之间的虚假相关性，是使文本嵌入适用于集体决策中偏好建模的关键。合成数据训练可以有效实现这一目标。

Abstract: Modern AI is opening the door to collective decision-making in which participants express their views as free-form text rather than voting on a fixed set of candidates. A natural idea is to embed these opinions in a vector space so that the substantial literature on facility location problems and fair clustering can be brought to bear. But standard text embeddings measure semantic similarity, whereas distances in facility location problems and fair clustering require what we call \textit{preferential similarity}: a participant's agreement with a piece of text should be inversely related to their distance from it. Off-the-shelf embeddings inherit a coarse preference signal through a correlation between semantic and preferential similarity, but fail to capture preferences when the correlation breaks. We formalize this as an invariance problem: text embedding models encode both a preference-relevant signal (stance and values) and semantic nuisance (style and wording), and the two are observationally correlated, so a geometry that relies on nuisance can appear preference-correct even when it is not. We show that synthetic training data designed to break this correlation provably shifts the optimal scorer away from nuisance-dominated cosine and significantly improves preference prediction across 11 online deliberation datasets.

</details>


### [163] [On Distinguishing Capability Elicitation from Capability Creation in Post-Training: A Free-Energy Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08368)
*Yuhao Li,Shengchao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了后训练研究中应区分"能力elicitation"（能力引出）和"能力创造"（能力创建）的概念，并引入"可访问支持集"来量化模型在有限预算下实际能产生的行为范围。SFT和RL本质上都是对预训练参考分布进行重新加权，区别仅在于使用的信号不同（演示信号 vs 奖励信号）。核心问题不是选择SFT还是RL，而是训练过程是在已有行为空间内重新加权，还是通过搜索、交互、工具使用或新信息扩展了模型可达的行为空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后训练研究常将SFT简单视为模仿、RL视为发现，这种二分法过于粗糙。作者认为需要更精确的概念框架来区分训练是"引出已有能力"还是"创造新能力"，以指导后训练研究的正确方向。

Method: 引入"可访问支持集"（accessible support）的概念来操作化能力elicitation与能力创造的区分；通过自由能视角（free-energy view）统一解释SFT和RL，将两者都视为对预训练参考分布的重加权，区别在于使用的信号不同（演示信号vs奖励信号）。

Result: SFT和RL在更新幅度接近基础模型时，主要效果都是局部重加权而非能力创造。后训练研究的核心问题不在于形式（SFT/RL），而在于训练是在已有可访问支持集内重加权，还是通过搜索、交互、工具使用或新信息扩展了该支持集。

Conclusion: 后训练研究应超越SFT vs RL的二元对立，关注更根本的区分：能力elicitation（支持集内重加权）vs 能力创造（支持集扩展）。这为设计更有效的后训练方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Debates about large language model post-training often treat supervised fine-tuning (SFT) as imitation and reinforcement learning (RL) as discovery. But this distinction is too coarse. What matters is whether a training procedure increases the probability of behaviors the pretrained model could already produce, or whether it changes what the model can practically reach. We argue that post-training research should distinguish between capability elicitation and capability creation. We make this distinction operational by introducing the notion of accessible support: the set of behaviors that a model can practically produce under finite budgets. Post-training that reweights behaviors within this support is capability elicitation; whereas changing the support itself corresponds to capability creation. We develop this argument through a free-energy view of post-training. SFT and RL can both be seen as reweighting a pretrained reference distribution, only with different external signals. Demonstration signals define low-energy behavior for SFT, and reward signals define low-energy behavior for RL. When the update remains close to the base model, the main effect is local reweighting, not capability creation. Within this framework, the central question is no longer whether post-training is framed as SFT or RL, but whether it reweights behaviors already within reach, or instead expands the model's reachable behavioral space through search, interaction, tool use, or the incorporation of new information.

</details>


### [164] [SkillLens: Adaptive Multi-Granularity Skill Reuse for Cost-Efficient LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08386)
*Yongliang Miao,Ziyang Yu,Liang Zhao,Bowen Zhu,Hasibul Haque*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillLens提出了一种分层技能进化框架，将技能组织为策略-策略-过程-原语四层图结构，通过混合粒度检索和自适应路由（接受/分解/重写/跳过）实现高效技能复用，在MuLocbench和ALFWorld上分别提升6.31%的Acc@1和6.31%的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能库系统将技能视为扁平、单一分辨率的提示块，导致粗粒度注入引入无关或误导性上下文，而重写整个技能成本高昂且不必要，存在相关性与成本之间的矛盾。

Method: SkillLens构建四层技能图（策略-策略-过程-原语），通过语义检索种子技能、基于度修正随机游走扩展技能图、利用验证器判断每个访问单元是否接受/分解/重写/跳过，实现混合粒度检索与自适应路由，并通过进化更新规则持续优化技能和验证器。

Result: 在MuLocbench上Bug定位Acc@1提升6.31个百分点，在ALFWorld上将智能体成功率从45.00%提升至51.31%，且理论分析证明混合粒度自适应在稀疏误匹配假设下具有次线性成本。

Conclusion: 分层技能进化框架能有效平衡技能复用的相关性与成本，混合粒度自适应路由策略具备理论保证和实际性能优势。

Abstract: Skill libraries have become a practical way for LLM agents to reuse procedural experience across tasks. However, existing systems typically treat skills as flat, single-resolution prompt blocks. This creates a tension between relevance and cost: injecting coarse skills can introduce irrelevant or misleading context, while rewriting entire skills is expensive and often unnecessary. We propose SkillLens, a hierarchical skill-evolution framework that organizes skills into a four-layer graph of policies, strategies, procedures, and primitives, and retrieves them at mixed granularity. Given a task, SkillLens first retrieves semantically relevant skill seeds, expands them through degree-corrected random walk over the skill graph, and then uses a verifier to decide whether each visited unit should be accepted, decomposed, rewritten, or skipped. This enables the agent to reuse compatible subskills directly while adapting only locally mismatched components. To improve the system over time, SkillLens further refines multi-granularity skills and verifier in order to improve its routing decisions. We provide theoretical analysis showing that mixed-granularity adaptation incurs sublinear cost under sparse mismatch assumptions and that the evolutionary update rule monotonically improves the validation objective until a local optimum. Across MuLocbench and ALFWorld, SkillLens consistently improves over strong skill-based baselines, achieving up to a 6.31 percentage-point Acc@1 gain for bug localization and raising agent success rate from 45.00% to 51.31%.

</details>


### [165] [PLACO: A Multi-Stage Framework for Cost-Effective Performance in Human-AI Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08388)
*Pranavkumar Mallela,Vinay Kumar,Shashi Shekhar Jha,Shweta Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对分类任务中人类与AI模型的团队协作，提出了一种基于贝叶斯规则的组合方法，利用人类类别级校准概率和模型实例级校准概率，在条件独立性假设下将确定性标注者（人类）与概率性标注者（分类模型）的输出进行有效融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类任务中，人类和AI模型各自存在局限，单一依靠任何一方都无法达到最优性能，因此需要有效的Human-AI团队协作方法来融合两者的输出。

Method: 基于贝叶斯规则，在人类和模型输出关于真实标签条件独立的假设下，利用人类类别级校准概率和模型实例级校准概率，将确定性标注者（人类）与概率性标注者（分类模型）的输出进行组合。

Result: 提出了一种结合人类（确定性标签）和模型（概率性标签）输出的组合方法，能够有效提升分类任务的整体表现。

Conclusion: 在分类任务中，通过贝叶斯方法融合人类和模型的输出，能够实现比单独使用任何一方更优的性能，为Human-AI团队协作提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Human-AI teams play a pivotal role in improving overall system performance when neither the human nor the model can achieve such performance on their own. With the advent of powerful and accessible Generative AI models, several mundane tasks have morphed into Human-AI team tasks. From writing essays to developing advanced algorithms, humans have found that using AI assistance has led to an accelerated work pace like never before. In classification tasks, where the final output is a single hard label, it is crucial to address the combination of human and model output. Prior work elegantly solves this problem using Bayes rule, using the assumption that human and model output are conditionally independent given the ground truth. Specifically, it discusses a combination method to combine a single deterministic labeler (the human) and a probabilistic labeler (the classifier model) using the model's instance-level and the human's class-level calibrated probabilities.

</details>


### [166] [Belief or Circuitry? Causal Evidence for In-Context Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08405)
*Katharine Kowalyshyn,Timothy Duggan,Daniel Little,Michael C Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过图随机游走任务探究LLM上下文学习的机制，发现模型同时编码全局拓扑结构和局部转移模式，支持双机制（结构推理+归纳电路）并行运作的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在上下文学习中究竟是通过匹配近期token的模式（局部机制）还是通过推断潜在结构（全局机制）来学习，解决这一争议性问题。

Method: 使用两种竞争图结构上的随机游走任务作为探测手段；通过PCA重建内部表示结构；利用残差流激活修补和图表差异引导进行因果干预实验。

Result: 在中间混合比例下，两种图拓扑被同时编码在正交主子空间中；后期层的激活修补几乎完全转移了干净图的偏好；线性引导能按预期方向移动预测，且在范数匹配和标签打乱的控制条件下失效。

Conclusion: 单一的局部机制或全局机制均不足以解释LLM的上下文学习行为，最符合双机制并行运作的解释，即真正的结构推理与归纳电路同时发挥作用。

Abstract: How do LLMs learn in-context? Is it by pattern-matching recent tokens, or by inferring latent structure? We probe this question using a toy graph random-walk across two competing graph structures. This task's answer is, in principle, decidable: either the model tracks global topology, or it copies local transitions. We present two lines of evidence that neither account alone is sufficient. First, reconstructing the internal representation structure via PCA reveals that at intermediate mixture ratios, both graph topologies are encoded in orthogonal principal subspaces simultaneously. This pattern is difficult to reconcile with purely local transition copying. Second, residual-stream activation patching and graph-difference steering causally intervene on this graph-family signal: late-layer patching almost fully transfers the clean graph preference, while linear steering moves predictions in the intended direction and fails under norm-matched and label-shuffled controls. Taken together, our findings are most consistent with a dual-mechanism account in which genuine structure inference and induction circuits operate in parallel.

</details>


### [167] [Playing games with knowledge: AI-Induced delusions need game theoretic interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08409)
*Will Beaumaster,Paul Schrater*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对话AI作为知识接口存在根本缺陷：谄媚的聊天机器人即使在理性主体中也会诱发认知固化和妄想信念螺旋。本文将其形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈，提出通过"认知中介"机制设计打破混同均衡，利用信念版本化系统实现分离均衡，将螺旋率降低48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话AI作为知识接口存在谄媚的系统性缺陷，导致理性用户也会陷入认知固化和妄想信念螺旋。现有研究多聚焦于模型层面的对齐，忽视了从用户驱动搜索到策略性重复沟通这一范式转变带来的系统性问题。

Method: 将问题形式化为Crawford-Sobel廉价谈话博弈模型，提出推理时机制设计干预——"认知中介"（Epistemic Mediator），通过引入认知摩擦（代价信号）打破混同均衡；提出受git启发的认知元记忆系统"信念版本化"（Belief Versioning），在检测到验证寻求型抵抗时存储健康信念并支持回滚。

Result: 模拟实验表明，该干预实现了分离均衡，将信念螺旋率差异提升48倍，同时通过了学习保留标准。

Conclusion: 认知安全本质上是战略信息环境设计问题，而非简单的模型对齐问题。

Abstract: Conversational AI has a fundamental flaw as a knowledge interface: sycophantic chatbots induce epistemic entrenchment and delusional belief spirals even in rational agents. We propose the problem does not stem from the AI model, rooted instead in a systemic consequence of the paradigm shift from user-driven knowledge search to users and agents engaged in strategic, repeated-play communication. We formalize the problem as a Crawford-Sobel cheap talk game, where costless user signals induce a pooling equilibrium. Agents optimized for user satisfaction produce sycophantic strategies that provide identical reinforcement across user types with opposite epistemic incentives: exploratory ``Growth-seekers'' ($θ_G$) and confirmatory ``Validation-seekers'' ($θ_V$). Under repeated play, this identification failure creates a coordination trap -- analogous to a Prisoner's Dilemma -- where locally rational feedback loops drive users toward pathologically certain false beliefs. We propose an inference-time mechanism design intervention called an Epistemic Mediator that breaks this pooling equilibrium by introducing a costly signal (epistemic friction), forcing type revelation based on users' asymmetric cognitive costs for processing resistance. A key contribution is Belief Versioning, a git-inspired epistemic meta-memory system that stores healthy beliefs and rollbacks when validation-seeking resistance is detected. In simulation, this intervention achieves a separating equilibrium achieving a $48\times$ differential in spiral rates while passing a learning preservation criterion), evidence that epistemic safety in AI is fundamentally a problem of strategic information environment design rather than simple model alignment.

</details>


### [168] [Political Plasticity: An Analysis of Ideological Adaptability in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08415)
*Bruno Bianchi,Diego Tiscornia,Matias Travizano,Ariel Futoransky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究提出了"政治可塑性"概念，定义模型根据用户上下文调整其政治立场的能力。通过200个政治问题测试发现：系统提示效果有限，而用户提示（尤其是少样本示例）能显著改变模型立场；大型新模型在自由经济轴上表现出一致的可塑性；问题格式反转实验揭示了反直觉偏移，暗示数据泄露；多语言实验显示细微但显著的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM的内在政治偏见，但缺乏对模型根据用户上下文动态调整其政治立场这一能力（即"政治可塑性"）的系统研究。

Method: 基于Lester (1996)框架构建200个政治问题（涵盖经济自由和个人自由两轴），设计简化/主题化的系统提示和带少样本的用户提示，实施多语言实验和问题格式反转验证实验。

Result: 系统提示效果有限，用户提示能显著引发模型意识形态偏移（尤其对大型新模型的自由经济轴）；问题反转实验发现反直觉偏移，暗示数据泄漏；多语言实验显示语言间存在细微但显著的差异。

Conclusion: 小型/老模型表现出有限或不稳定的政治可塑性，而更新的前沿模型展现出可靠且符合预期的适应性。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), a significant area of research has focused on their intrinsic biases, particularly in political discourse. This study investigates a different but related concept, "political plasticity", which is defined as the capacity of models to adapt their responses based on the user supplied context. To analyze this, a testing framework was developed using an expanded corpus of 200 politically-oriented questions across economic and personal freedom axes, based on a prior framework by Lester (1996). The study explored several methods to induce political bias, including simplified and topic-based system prompts, as well as user prompts with few-shot examples. The results show that while system prompts were largely ineffective, user prompts successfully elicited significant ideological shifts, particularly along the Economic Freedom axis in larger and newer models. Through a validation experiment, we examined whether models answer questionnaires by recognizing the underlying question format. Inverting the sense of the questions revealed unexpected, counter-intuitive shifts in most models, suggesting potential data leakage. Finally, we also analyzed how model plasticity varies when the experiment is conducted in different languages. The results reveal subtle yet notable shifts across each of the analyzed languages. Overall, our results indicate that small and older LLMs exhibit limited or unstable political plasticity, whereas newer frontier models display reliable, expected adaptability.

</details>


### [169] [Alignment as Jurisprudence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08416)
*Nicholas Caputo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了法理学（法官如何正确裁判）与AI对齐（使AI模型符合人类价值观）之间的深层结构相似性，通过将德沃金的原则解释主义、桑斯坦的类比推理法等法学理论与宪法AI、基于案例推理等对齐方法进行对话，论证了两个领域可以相互启发、共同进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法理学与AI对齐看似遥远，但都致力于预测和塑造强大行为体（法官和AI）在未来未知情境中的决策，且都依赖语言的规范与解释。作者希望将两个领域直接对话，从彼此中汲取洞见。

Method: 通过对比分析法学理论（德沃金的原则解释主义、桑斯坦的类比推理实证主义法学）与AI对齐方法（宪法AI、基于案例推理），展示规则与案例在微调对齐中的相互作用，并探讨AI如何帮助理解并改进法律运作。

Result: 揭示了一种更精细的、受法律启发的对齐方法，强调规则与案例的互动在微调中的价值；同时指出AI可以为法律如何运作及其改进提供新的理解。

Conclusion: AI系统与法律都应赋能人类，扩展其能力与实现目标的可能性。随着AI能力提升及法律理论对法官约束的松动，两个领域的对话将愈发重要，有助于塑造更好的法律与AI系统。

Abstract: Jurisprudence, the study of how judges should properly decide cases, and alignment, the science of getting AI models to conform to human values, share a fundamental structure. These seemingly distant fields both seek to predict and shape how decisions by powerful actors, in one case judges and in the other increasingly powerful artificial intelligences, will be made in the unknown future. And they use similar tools of the specification and interpretation of language to try to accomplish those goals. The great debates of jurisprudence, about what the law is and what it should be, can provide insight into alignment, and lessons from what does and does not work in alignment can help make progress in jurisprudence.
  This essay puts the two fields directly into conversation. Drawing on leading accounts of jurisprudence, particularly Dworkin's principle-oriented interpretivism and Sunstein's positivist account of law as analogical reasoning, and on cutting-edge alignment approaches, namely Constitutional AI and case-based reasoning, it illustrates the value of a more sophisticated legally-inspired approach to the interplay of rules and cases in finetuning alignment and points to ways that AI can provide a better understanding of how the law works and how it can be improved by the introduction of AI. AI systems and the law should operate to empower people to act in the world, helping to expand their capabilities and the extent to which they are able to achieve their goals. As AI continues to improve in capacity, and as the constraints that legal theory places on human judges seem be coming undone, the conversation between these two fields will become increasingly essential and may help point to a better version of both.

</details>


### [170] [The Attacker in the Mirror: Breaking Self-Consistency in Safety via Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08427)
*Gabriele La Malfa,Emanuele La Malfa,Saar Cohen,Jie M. Zhang,Michael Luck,Michael Wooldridge,Elizabeth Black*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出锚定双策略自博弈（Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play），通过为攻击者和防御者训练独立的LoRA适配器（共享同一冻结基模型），解决了传统自博弈中参数共享导致攻击压力消失、策略收敛到平凡解（如始终拒绝）的理论与架构局限，在保持参数效率的同时显著提升了安全鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自博弈红队方法中，攻击者和防御者共享同一模型参数，这虽然提升了稳定性，但引入了两大根本性问题：（1）可达的纳什均衡集合包含始终拒绝等平凡策略，限制了实际应用；（2）参数共享导致动态过程坍缩为自一致性，攻击无法对防御者施加真正的对抗压力。

Method: 提出锚定双策略自博弈（ABSP），在冻结的基础模型上为攻击者和防御者分别训练独立的LoRA适配器，实现明确的角色分离。这种方法既保持了优化的稳定性（通过共享基模型），又保留了对抗压力（通过独立适配器），同时相比全参数微调实现了最高100倍的参数效率提升。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-{3B,7B,14B}-IT模型上的安全基准评估显示：ABSP相比传统自博弈微调模型在安全性上持续提升，且不损失推理能力。跨博弈实验进一步表明，ABSP的攻击者和防御者在对抗防御和安全性方面均优于自博弈。

Conclusion: 锚定双策略自博弈通过角色分离的LoRA架构有效解决了自博弈红队中因参数共享导致的理论与实际问题，在参数效率、安全鲁棒性和推理保持之间取得了更好的平衡。

Abstract: Self-play red team is an established approach to improving AI safety in which different instances of the same model play attacker and defender roles in a zero-sum game, i.e., where the attacker tries to jailbreak the defender; if self-play converges to a Nash equilibrium, the model is guaranteed to respond safely within the settings of the game. Although the parameter sharing enforced by the use of the same model for the two roles improves stability and performance, it introduces fundamental theoretical and architectural limitations. We show that the set of Nash equilibria that can be reached corresponds to a broad class of behaviours that includes trivial always refuse strategies and oracle-like defenders, thus limiting practical applicability. We then show that when attacker and defender share and update the same base model, the dynamics collapse to self-consistency, so that attacks do not enforce adversarial pressure on the defender. In response, we propose Anchored Bipolicy Self-Play, which trains distinct role-specific LoRA adapters on top of a frozen base model, thereby maintaining stable optimisation while preserving adversarial pressure through explicit role separation. In relation to standard self-play, we show up to 100x greater parameter efficiency than finetuning and consistent improvements in safety compared to self-play fine-tuned models. We evaluate on Qwen2.5-{3B, 7B,14B}-IT models across widely used safety benchmarks, showing improved robustness without loss of reasoning ability. Cross-play experiments further show that our attacker and defender models are superior to self-play in terms of adversarial defence and safety.

</details>


### [171] [Measuring What Matters: Benchmarking Generative, Multimodal, and Agentic AI in Healthcare](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08445)
*Prasanna Desikan,Harshit Rajgarhia,Shivali Dalmia,Ananya Mantravadi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 当前医疗AI基准测试主要衡量模型的知识水平，而非其在真实临床环境中的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性，导致高分模型在真实场景中性能严重下降（如文档任务0.74-0.85、临床决策支持0.61-0.76、行政工作流仅0.53-0.63），迫切需要一种系统的基准设计框架来弥合测试性能与实际临床效用之间的鸿沟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准测试缺乏系统性方法衡量模型在真实临床环境中的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性，且基准测试分数与实际临床部署性能之间存在巨大差距，高基准分数给部署就绪性带来虚假安全感，亟待建立原则性的基准设计框架。

Method: 通过分析现有基准测试的局限性（基于临时性数据集构建、优化狭隘任务性能），对比前沿模型在标准考试（接近满分）与真实临床任务（文档0.74-0.85、临床决策支持0.61-0.76、行政工作流0.53-0.63）之间的性能差距，揭示基准设计框架缺失的根本问题。

Result: 前沿模型在医学执照考试中表现接近完美，但在真实临床任务中性能大幅下降：文档任务0.74-0.85、临床决策支持0.61-0.76、行政与工作流任务仅0.53-0.63（基于MedHelm评测）。

Conclusion: 没有原则性的基准设计框架，该领域无法判断临床性能差是模型能力限制还是测量方法失败所致，迫切需要系统性方法来衡量真实条件下的可靠性、安全性和临床相关性。

Abstract: AI models are increasingly deployed in live clinical environments where they must perform reliably across complex, high-stakes workflows that standard training and validation datasets were never designed to capture. Evaluating these systems requires benchmarks: structured combinations of tasks, datasets, and metrics that enable reproducible, comparable measurement of what a model can do. The central challenge in healthcare AI is not performance alone, but the absence of systematic methods to measure reliability, safety, and clinical relevance under real-world conditions. Most existing benchmarks test what a model knows; too few test whether it can perform reliably and without failing across the full complexity of real clinical tasks. Current benchmarks have accumulated through ad hoc dataset construction optimized for narrow task performance: frontier models achieve near-perfect scores on medical licensing examinations, but when evaluated across real clinical tasks, performance degrades sharply, scoring 0.74--0.85 on documentation, 0.61--0.76 on clinical decision support, and only 0.53--0.63 on administrative and workflow tasks \cite{medhelm}. High benchmark scores give a false sense of deployment readiness, and the gap between performance and utility widens precisely as AI systems take on more consequential clinical roles. Without a principled framework for benchmark design, the field cannot determine whether poor clinical performance reflects model limitations or failures in how performance is being measured.

</details>


### [172] [LLM-guided Semi-Supervised Approaches for Social Media Crisis Data Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08448)
*Jacob Ativo,Bharaneeshwar Balasubramaniyam,Anh Tran,Khushboo Gupta,Hongmin Li,Doina Caragea,Cornelia Caragea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对大语言模型引导的半监督学习方法在灾难相关推文分类任务中进行了实证评估，发现LG-CoTrain在低资源场景下显著优于经典半监督方法，且紧凑的半监督模型在某些情况下优于大规模零样本LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用LLM引导的半监督学习来增强社交媒体数据分析在灾害管理中的应用，解决低资源场景下标注数据稀缺的问题。

Method: 对比两种LLM辅助的半监督方法（VerifyMatch和LLM引导的协同训练LG-CoTrain）与经典半监督基线，在5、10、25个每类标注样本的低资源设置下进行危机推文分类实验。

Result: LG-CoTrain在低资源设置下获得最高的平均Macro F1值；VerifyMatch性能接近且具有良好的校准特性；随标注样本增加，性能差距缩小，Self Training成为强基线；紧凑半监督模型在某些情况下超越大型零样本LLM。

Conclusion: LLM引导的半监督学习能够将知识从大模型迁移至更小、更易部署的模型，为实际灾难响应应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Semi-supervised learning approaches have been investigated as a means to enhance the analysis of social media data in disaster management contexts. In this work, we present the first empirical evaluation of large language model (LLM) guided semi-supervised learning for crisis related tweet classification. We compare two recent LLM assisted semi-supervised methods, VerifyMatch and LLM guided Co-Training ( LG-CoTrain), against established semi-supervised baselines. Our results show that LG-CoTrain significantly outperforms classical semi-supervised approaches in low resource settings with 5, 10 and 25 labeled examples per class, achieving the highest averaged Macro F1 across events. VerifyMatch achieves competitive performance while also demonstrating strong calibration properties. As the number of labeled examples increases, the performance gap narrows and Self Training emerges as a strong baseline. We further observe that compact semi-supervised models can, in some cases, outperform very large LLMs operating in zero-shot settings. This finding highlights the potential of transferring knowledge from LLMs into smaller and more deployable models through LLM guided semi-supervised learning, offering a practical pathway for real world disaster response applications. Our project repository on Github is here.

</details>


### [173] [Mid-Training with Self-Generated Data Improves Reinforcement Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08472)
*Aswin RRV,Jacob Dineen,Divij Handa,Mihir Parmar,Ben Zhou,Swaroop Mishra,Chitta Baral*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在强化学习（RL）之前，利用基于乔治·波利亚问题解决方法的自生成多样化数据进行中期训练，使语言模型掌握多种解题方法，从而提升后续RL在数学推理、代码生成等任务上的效果。论文提供了理论分析和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL在LLM中的效果受限于训练数据的多样性和性质。推理问题通常有多种解决路径，若训练数据只覆盖有限范围的方法，会限制RL的有效性。因此，需要让模型在RL前就接触多种解题方法。

Method: 采用自举式数据生成框架，依据George Polya的问题解决理论，为每个问题生成多个正确答案变体，然后在中期训练阶段进行微调，再进入RL阶段。理论上从策略梯度更新角度解释了中期训练如何激励多种方法的组合。

Result: 使用中期训练数据初始化的RL模型在多个数学推理基准、代码生成和叙事推理等OOD任务上取得了一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 语言模型通过学习多种问题解决方法（通过自生成数据实现）能够有效辅助后续的强化学习训练。

Abstract: The effectiveness of Reinforcement Learning (RL) in Large Language Models (LLMs) depends on the nature and diversity of the data used before and during RL. In particular, reasoning problems can often be approached in multiple ways that rely on different forms of reasoning, and exposure to only a limited range of such approaches in the training data may limit the effectiveness of RL. Motivated by this, we investigate using diverse self-generated data during mid-training as an intermediate step before RL training. Specifically, we adopt a bootstrapped data-generation framework guided by George Polya's problem-solving approaches for generating multiple variants of correct answers for each question in the training data, and then perform fine-tuning. We first provide a theoretical perspective on how mid-training on such data improves RL and explain how policy-gradient updates can incentivize combining multiple approaches. We then empirically demonstrate that RL-trained models initialized with our mid-training data achieve consistent improvements across various mathematical reasoning benchmarks and other OOD tasks like code generation and narrative reasoning. Overall, our investigative study shows that a language model learning multiple problem-solving approaches, through self-generated data helps subsequent RL.

</details>


### [174] [Latent Personality Alignment: Improving Harmlessness Without Mentioning Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08496)
*Linh Le,David Williams-King,Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Aton Kamanda,Adam Oberman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出潜在人格对齐（LPA）方法，通过少于100个人格特质语句和潜在对抗训练，在无需有害样本的情况下，达到与150k+示例方法相当的鲁棒性，并将误分类率降低2.6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型对抗鲁棒性方法需要大量有害提示数据集（数千至数十万），但仍易受到新型攻击和分布偏移的影响，亟需更高效、泛化能力更强的防御方法。

Method: 提出潜在人格对齐（LPA），训练模型学习抽象人格特质而非具体有害行为，使用少于100个人格特质语句进行潜在对抗训练。

Result: LPA在攻击成功率上与基于150k+示例的方法相当；在六个危害基准上，对未见攻击分布的误分类率降低2.6倍；且保持更优的实用性。

Conclusion: 基于人格的对齐方法为以极低成本构建鲁棒防御提供了一种原则性方案，证明了其样本高效性和强泛化能力。

Abstract: Current adversarial robustness methods for large language models require extensive datasets of harmful prompts (thousands to hundreds of thousands of examples), yet remain vulnerable to novel attack vectors and distributional shifts. We propose Latent Personality Alignment (LPA), a sample-efficient defense that achieves robustness by training models on abstract personality traits rather than specific harmful behaviors. Using fewer than 100 trait statements and latent adversarial training, LPA achieves comparable attack success rates to methods trained on 150k+ examples, while maintaining superior utility. Critically, LPA generalizes better to unseen attack distributions, reducing misclassification rates by 2.6x compared to baseline across six harm benchmarks -- without ever seeing harmful examples during training. Our results demonstrate that personality-based alignment offers a principled approach to building robust defenses with minimal cost.

</details>


### [175] [OracleTSC: Oracle-Informed Reward Hurdle and Uncertainty Regularization for Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08516)
*Darryl Jacob,Xinyu Liu,Muchao Ye,Xiaoyong Yuan,Pan He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OracleTSC通过奖励门槛机制和不确定性正则化，稳定了基于LLM的交通信号控制强化学习微调，使LLaMA3-8B模型在交通效率上实现75%行程时间减少和67%队列长度降低，同时保持可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于强化学习的交通信号控制系统是黑盒模型，缺乏可解释性；而大语言模型虽能提供自然语言推理，但其强化微调因反馈稀疏和延迟而不稳定，大多数动作对拥堵指标的改善微乎其微。

Method: 提出OracleTSC框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 奖励门槛机制——通过减去校准阈值来过滤弱学习信号；(2) 不确定性正则化——最大化所选响应的概率，促使采样输出之间的一致性决策。

Result: 在LibSignal基准上，OracleTSC使LLaMA3-8B模型实现75%行程时间减少和67%队列长度降低，保持自然语言可解释性；跨路口泛化中，在新结构路口上实现17%更低的行程时间和39%更低的队列长度，无需额外微调。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知的奖励塑形能够提高交通信号控制中强化微调的稳定性和有效性。

Abstract: Transparent decision-making is essential for traffic signal control (TSC) systems to earn public trust. However, traditional reinforcement learning-based TSC methods function as black boxes with limited interpretability. Although large language models (LLMs) can provide natural language reasoning, reinforcement finetuning for TSC remains unstable because feedback is sparse and delayed, while most actions produce only marginal changes in congestion metrics. We introduce OracleTSC, which stabilizes LLM-based TSC through two mechanisms: (1) a reward hurdle mechanism that filters weak learning signals by subtracting a calibrated threshold from environmental rewards, and (2) uncertainty regularization that maximizes the probability of the selected response to encourage consistent decisions across sampled outputs. Experiments on the LibSignal benchmark show that OracleTSC enables a compact LLaMA3-8B model to substantially improve traffic efficiency, achieving a 75% reduction in travel time and a 67% decrease in queue length compared with the pretrained baseline while preserving interpretability through natural language explanations. OracleTSC also demonstrates strong cross-intersection generalization: a policy trained on one intersection transfers to a structurally different intersection with 17% lower travel time and 39% lower queue length without additional finetuning. These results suggest that uncertainty-aware reward shaping can improve the stability and effectiveness of reinforcement fine-tuning for TSC.

</details>


### [176] [Results and Retrospective Analysis of the CODS 2025 AssetOpsBench Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08518)
*Dhaval Patel,Chathurangi Shyalika,Suryanarayana Reddy Yarrabothula,Ling Yue,Shuxin Lin,Nianjun Zhou,James Rayfield*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析CODS 2025 AssetOpsLive挑战赛的公开排行榜、隐藏评估、提交日志等多源数据，揭示了工业多智能体编排竞赛中评估设计对结论的影响，并提出了可扩展的评估改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛回顾能揭示排行榜衡量了什么、隐藏评估如何改变结论、以及哪些设计模式获得了奖励。本文旨在系统分析CODS 2025 AssetOpsLive挑战赛，以理解其评估机制的实际效果。

Method: 结合最终排名表、300次提交的服务器日志、149支队伍注册信息、最优提交导出、组织者获奖报告、配套系统论文以及经过验证的规划赛道源代码树等多源数据进行综合分析。

Result: 1) 公开规划排行榜饱和于72.73%，更丰富的提示无法提升该峰值；2) 隐藏评估改变结论：规划赛道公开与私有分数中度相关(r=0.69)，执行赛道呈负相关(r=-0.13)，部分45.45%公开执行系统在隐藏集上达63.64%；3) TMATCH项在官方综合评分中几乎无实际影响；4) 竞赛操作上基于账户但实质上是基于团队；5) 成功的执行方法主要改进护栏机制而非新颖的智能体架构。

Conclusion: 这些发现识别了评估所奖励的行为，并推动了尺度感知的综合评分、技能水平诊断以及版本化工件发布等改进方向。

Abstract: Competition retrospectives are useful when they explain what a leaderboard measured, how hidden evaluation changed conclusions, and which design patterns were rewarded. We revisit the CODS 2025 \assetopslive{} challenge, a privacy-aware Codabench competition on industrial multi-agent orchestration built on \assetops{}. We combine final rank sheets, a 300-submission server log, 149-team registrations, best-submission exports, the organizer winners report, the companion \assetopslive{} system paper, and verified planning-track source trees. Five results stand out. First, the public planning leaderboard saturates at 72.73\%, and richer prompts do not improve that peak. Second, hidden evaluation changes the story: public and private scores correlate moderately in planning ($r{=}0.69$) but negatively in execution ($r{=}{-}0.13$), with several 45.45\% public execution systems reaching 63.64\% on the hidden set. Third, the \tmatch{} term is numerically almost inert in the official composite -- combined on a 0--1 scale with 0--100 percentage scores, it contributes at most 0.05 points per track, and rescaling would swap the top two teams. Fourth, the competition is operationally account-based but substantively team-based: 149 registered teams reduce to 24 with non-zero public scores and 11 fully ranked, while 52.3\% of deduplicated registrations list multiple usernames. Fifth, successful execution methods mostly improve guardrails -- response selection, contamination cleanup, fallback, and context control -- rather than novel agent architectures. These findings identify which behaviors the evaluation rewarded, and motivate scale-aware composites, skill-level diagnostics, and versioned artifact release.

</details>


### [177] [Human-LLM Dialogue Improves Diagnostic Accuracy in Emergency Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08533)
*Burcu Sayin,Ngoc Vo Hong,Ipek Baris Schlicht,Jacopo Staiano,Pasquale Minervini,Sara Allievi,Nicola Susca,Nicola Osti,Alberto Maino,Vito Racanelli,Andrea Passerini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedSyn是一个让医生在急诊场景下与LLM进行交互式诊断的系统。7名医生在52个MIMIC-IV病例上的实验表明，AI辅助显著提升了住院医师的诊断正确率（Hard-case从0.589提升到0.734），并缩小了不同经验水平医生之间的诊断差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在医疗诊断基准上取得了进展，但缺乏其在真实临床工作流中作为交互式辅助工具的证据，特别是在急诊医学这种需要快速准确决策的场景下。

Method: 构建MedSyn系统，允许医生在仅看到主诉的情况下，向能够访问完整临床记录的LLM进行迭代式提问。7名医生（3名资深，4名住院医师）在52个MIMIC-IV病例上分别完成基线和AI辅助两轮诊断，按病例难度分层。

Result: 住院医师在困难病例上的正确率从0.589提升至0.734；标准化完全正确率有中等效应（Δ=0.092, p=0.071, d=0.47）；标准化任意匹配准确率提升0.156（p<0.0001）；住院医师F1增益最大（Δ=0.138, p<0.0001）；跨专业一致性提升（Δ=0.145, p<0.0001）；对话分析显示不同经验医生采用不同提问策略。

Conclusion: 交互式LLM辅助能够有效增强急诊诊断推理能力，尤其对住院医师帮助显著，并能提升不同经验水平医生之间诊断的一致性。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making in emergency medicine demands rapid, accurate diagnoses under uncertainty. Despite benchmark progress, evidence for LLMs as interactive aids in live physician workflows remains sparse. MedSyn lets physicians iteratively query an LLM provided with the full clinical record while initially viewing only the chief complaint. Seven physicians (three seniors, four residents) completed baseline and AI-assisted sessions across 52 MIMIC-IV cases stratified by difficulty. Blinded evaluation showed residents' Hard-case correctness rose from 0.589 to 0.734; difficulty-standardised completely-correct rates confirmed a medium effect (Δ = 0.092; p = 0.071; d = 0.47). Automated metrics corroborated these gains: standardised any-match accuracy improved by 0.156 (p < 0.0001), and residents showed the largest F1 gain (Δ = 0.138; p < 0.0001). Dialogue analysis revealed expertise-dependent strategies (seniors asked targeted, hypothesis-driven questions; residents relied on broader queries) and cross-expertise concordance increased (Δ = 0.145; p < 0.0001). Interactive LLM support meaningfully enhances diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [178] [Log analysis is necessary for credible evaluation of AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08545)
*Peter Kirgis,Sayash Kapoor,Stephan Rabanser,Nitya Nadgir,Cozmin Ududec,Magda Dubois,JJ Allaire,Conrad Stosz,Marius Hobbhahn,Jacob Steinhardt,Arvind Narayanan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出智能体基准测试仅报告最终结果（通过/失败）会威胁评估可信度，提出通过日志分析（系统追踪和分析AI智能体的输入、执行和输出）来解决这些问题，并提出了威胁分类法、指导原则，以及在tau-Bench Airline上的实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体基准测试仅报告最终结果（通过/失败）存在三个可信度威胁：分数被基准测试捷径和伪影扭曲导致能力误判；基准性能因脚手架局限性和重复失败模式无法预测实际效用；能力分数可能掩盖智能体的危险或灾难性行为。

Method: (1) 提出了通过日志分析识别出的可信评估威胁分类法；(2) 制定了一套日志分析的指导原则；(3) 在tau-Bench Airline基准上进行了实证验证。

Result: 在tau-Bench Airline上的实验表明，pass^5的性能被低估了近50%，并揭示了结果指标无法发现的部署失败模式。

Conclusion: 日志分析对于克服可信评估威胁、促进可信的智能体评估是必要的，并向基准创建者、模型开发者、独立评估者和部署者等不同利益相关者提出了实用的建议。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks typically report only final outcomes: pass or fail. This threatens evaluation credibility in three ways. First, scores may be inflated or deflated by shortcuts and benchmark artifacts, misrepresenting capability. Second, benchmark performance may fail to predict real-world utility due to scaffold limitations and recurring failure modes. Finally, capability scores may conceal dangerous or catastrophic actions taken by the agent. We argue that log analysis -- the systematic tracking and analysis of the inputs, execution, and outputs of an AI agent -- is necessary to overcome these validity threats and promote credible agent evaluation. In this paper, we (1) present a taxonomy of threats to credible evaluation documented through log analysis, and (2) develop a set of guiding principles for log analysis. We illustrate these principles on tau-Bench Airline, revealing that pass^5 performance was under-elicited by nearly 50% and surfacing deployment failure modes invisible to outcome metrics. We conclude with pragmatic recommendations to increase uptake of log analysis, directed at diverse stakeholders including benchmark creators, model developers, independent evaluators, and deployers.

</details>


### [179] [Evaluating Developmental Cognition Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08549)
*Xiao Xiao,Hayoun Noh,Mar Gonzalez-Franco*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入发展性句子完成测试（DSCT），一种20题的自填式工具，用于在对话AI中捕捉用户的发展阶段信号。实验表明，前沿LLM在模拟数据上能高准确率恢复阶段标签，在真实人类响应上与人类一致性中等，且不同模型家族回答DSCT提示时显示出稳定的阶段特征。核心结论是：阶段感知对话AI的瓶颈不在于分类器精度，而在于从引发文本中获取发展信号的可获得性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话AI个性化主要围绕用户偏好、历史、目标和知识，但较少关注用户如何解释和接受模型输出以构建自身现实。作者借鉴Kegan的建构-发展理论作为补充视角，但现有发展阶段评估方法（专家访谈、句子完成测试）不可扩展、专有、冗长或侵入性强。因此，需要一种可扩展、适合LLM评估的发展阶段信号捕捉方法。

Method: 1. 设计了20题的DSCT（发展性句子完成测试），用于在自填文本中引发发展性信号。2. 在三种响应场景中测试LLM恢复阶段信号的能力：模拟角色、真实人类受访者、默认模型生成答案。3. 使用前沿LLM分类器评估信号恢复准确率，并分析不同模型家族间的稳定阶段差异。

Result: 1. 在模拟角色上，前沿模型以高准确率恢复模拟器预设的阶段标签。2. 在真实人类DSCT回答上，人-LLM一致性为中等（fair），邻近阶段一致性远强于精确匹配。3. 当LLM无条件回答DSCT时，不同模型家族表现出稳定的阶段差异，更大更新的模型倾向于生成评分更高的文本。4. 阶段信号在合成响应中比人类书面文本更干净。

Conclusion: 阶段感知对话AI的核心约束不在于分类器精度，而在于从引发的文本中获取发展信号的可获得性。DSCT为将建构-发展理论视角引入LLM评估提供了可行路径，但真实人类数据上的信号噪声仍是主要挑战。

Abstract: Conversational AI is increasingly personalized around users' preferences, histories, goals, and knowledge, but much less around how users interpret and take up model outputs to construct and understand their reality. We draw on Robert Kegan's constructive-developmental theory as a complementary lens on this dimension. Existing methods for assessing developmental stage in the Keganian tradition rely either on expert interviews that do not scale or on sentence-completion instruments that are proprietary, lengthy, or invasive. To make this perspective tractable for LLM evaluation, we introduce the Developmental Sentence Completion Test (DSCT), a 20-item instrument designed to elicit developmental signal in self-administered text. Throughout, we treat the resulting labels as characterizations of stage-like structure in elicited responses, not as validated person-level developmental stage. We then ask how much of that signal can be recovered by LLMs across three elicited response regimes: simulated personas, real human respondents, and default model-generated answers.
  On simulated personas, top frontier models recover simulator-intended labels with high accuracy. On real human DSCT responses, human-LLM agreement is fair, with much stronger within-neighborhood than exact agreement. Finally, when LLMs answer DSCT prompts without persona-conditioning, their responses exhibit stable stage-like differences across model families, with larger and newer models tending to generate higher-rated text. These results suggest that stage-conditioned signal is cleaner in synthetic responses than in human-written DSCT text, and that the core constraint for stage-aware conversational AI is not classifier accuracy alone, but the availability of developmental signal from elicited text.

</details>


### [180] [Biological Plausibility and Representational Alignment of Feedback Alignment in Convolutional Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08564)
*Jake Lance,Larry Kieu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文评估了五种学习算法（包括修正的反馈对齐和标准反向传播）在相同卷积架构和CIFAR-10数据集上的表现，从生物合理性、可解释性和计算复杂度三个维度进行比较。结果表明修正的反馈对齐算法虽然权重更新机制与反向传播根本不同，但能够收敛到与反向传播结构相似的内部表征，其功能成功可能源于模仿反向传播的表征几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反馈对齐算法作为反向传播的生物合理替代方案，在卷积架构上扩展失败，而现有修正方案又牺牲了生物合理性。因此需要在同一卷积架构下系统比较不同学习算法，探究修正FA为何能成功以及其与BP的表征相似性。

Method: 在相同卷积架构下使用CIFAR-10数据集，对五种学习算法（包括修正FA和标准BP）进行三方面比较分析：生物合理性、可解释性和计算复杂度。

Result: 修正的FA算法能够收敛到与BP结构相似的内部表征；修正FA的功能成功可能源于其模仿BP表征几何的能力；尽管权重更新机制根本不同，但表征收敛性相似。

Conclusion: 修正的FA算法在生物合理性与功能性之间取得了更好的平衡；其成功的关键在于能够复现BP的表征几何结构，而非依赖于相同的权重更新机制。

Abstract: The feedback alignment (FA) algorithm offers a biologically plausible alternative to backpropagation (BP) for training neural networks yet notably fails to scale to convolutional architectures. Modifications have been proposed to address this limitation, but at questionable cost to biological plausibility. In this paper, we evaluate five learning algorithms including modified FA and standard BP, applied to the same convolutional architecture with the CIFAR-10 dataset. We provide a tripartite comparative analysis focusing on biological plausibility, interpretability, and computational complexity. Our results indicate that modified FA algorithms converge on internal representations that are structurally similar to those produced by backpropagation. In particular, it appears the functional success of modified FA algorithms may be rooted in their ability to mimic the representational geometry of backpropagation, converging on similar representations despite relying on fundamentally different weight update mechanisms.

</details>


### [181] [What Will Happen Next: Large Models-Driven Deduction for Emergency Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08599)
*Zhengqing Hu,Dong Chen,Junkun Yuan,Liang Liu,Hua Wang,Zhao Jin,Yingchaojie Feng,Wei Chen,Mingliang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出大模型驱动的世界线偏离系统（WLDS），利用大模型的随机生成能力、先验知识和跨域迁移能力，结合事实校准与逻辑校准机制，实现多领域应急事件实例的多样化推演与可视化，实验表明该系统能生成高精度、高保真的应急推演数据，并为应急决策提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统应急仿真方法依赖预设重现已发生事件，缺乏随机性和多样性，难以充分探索潜在风险；而应急事件实例稀缺，现有系统无法有效覆盖未知风险场景。

Method: 提出WLDS系统，利用大模型动态调整生成策略引入可控随机性；设计事实校准与逻辑校准机制保证推演过程的准确性与严谨性；引入交互模块自主选择推演方向以避免系统难以识别的幻觉；引入可视化模块实现图文结合的仿真推演。

Result: 在提出的应急事件推演（EID）基准数据集上，WLDS在多个特定领域实现了高精度、高保真的应急事件推演；能生成更多推演数据，为未来相似应急事件提供更好的决策支持。

Conclusion: 大模型驱动的世界线偏离系统能够有效克服传统仿真方法的局限性，通过可控随机性和跨域知识迁移实现多样化的应急事件推演，具有重要的应急管理应用价值。

Abstract: Traditional simulation methods reproduce occurred emergency instances through presetting to assist people in risk assessment and emergency decision-making. However, due to the lack of randomness and diversity, existing simulation systems struggle to fully explore the potential risk as emergency instances are scarce. In contrast, Large Models (LMs) can dynamically adjust generation strategies to introduce controllable randomness, while also possessing extensive prior knowledge and cross-domain knowledge transfer capabilities. Inspired by it, we propose the LMs-driven World Line Divergence System (WLDS), which enables diversified visualization and deduction of emergency instances in different domains. WLDS leverages LMs to deduce emergency instances in various development directions, and introduces the factual calibration and logical calibration mechanism to ensure factual accuracy and logical rigor during the deduction process. The interactive module can independently select deduction directions to avoid potential hallucinations that are difficult for the system to identify. Furthermore, by introducing the visualization module, WLDS forms simulation and deduction that combine text and images, which enhances interpretability. Extensive experiments conducted on the proposed Emergency Instances Deduction (EID) benchmark dataset demonstrate that WLDS achieves high-precision and high-fidelity simulation and deduction of emergency instances in multiple specific domains. Relevant experiments further demonstrate that WLDS can generate more emergency instances deduction data for users and provide support for better decision-making in similar emergency instances in the future.

</details>
